\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 30}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0323}{234}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L001}{}{}{psis nonnihil retardari necesse fuit. An vero totus error integri horae quadrantis huic causae}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L002}{}{}{sit adscribendus, affirmare non audeo; ipse enim al–Battānī, rudiori modo, tempora sua non}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L003}{}{}{accuratius quam per denorum minutorum intervalla adnotasse videtur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L004}{}{}{\Indent Demum \textit{magnitudines} eclipsium lunarium ab al–Battānī observatarum cum Oppolzeri}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L005}{}{}{supputationibus comparare iuvabit. Magnitudo eclipsis III, quam al–Battānī 10 digitorum aesti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L006}{}{}{mavit, fuit iuxta Oppolzer 11,2 digitorum. Magnitudo eclipsis IV, quam al–Battānī 12 digitorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L007}{}{}{vel paulo minorem fuisse vidit, e calculis Oppolzer nonnihilo maior, seu 12,6 digitorum inve-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L008}{}{}{nitur. — Magnitudines duarum eclipsium Solis, I et II, al–Battānī non tradidit. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{4.14mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-H09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L009}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{l}}) — \textit{Pag. 58, l. 1–4.}}}
\par\vspace{3.37mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L010}{}{}{\Indent [« \textit{Sol ad apogeum versus erat} ». — Nam in III eclipsi anomalia \SunSym{} 42° erat, in IV 52°.}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.144\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0323-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.836\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.164\FullSourceWidth}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.749}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L011}{}{}{\Indent « \textit{Luna eundem locum occupabat partibus 1\textsuperscript{p} $\qfrac{1}{2}$ circiter minorem}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L012}{}{}{\textit{quam distantiam mediam} ». — I. e. 1\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} $\qfrac{1}{2}$ circiter ex 60 partes in quas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L013}{}{}{media distantia (AE in figura pag. 51) Lunae coniunctae et oppositae Soli di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L014}{}{}{vidi statuitur. Re vera in III eclipsi anomalia vera Lunae in epicyclo 114° 16′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L015}{}{}{erat, in IV 111° 4′; et hae sunt quantitates anguli QAL in figura hoc loco}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L016}{}{}{descripta, ubi E terra est, A centrum epicycli, AE $=$ 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}, AL $=$ 5\textsuperscript{\textit{p}},247. Cal-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L017}{}{}{culus trianguli AEL, secundum III eclipsim, EL $=$58\textsuperscript{\textit{p}},04 praebet; secundum IV,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L018}{}{}{EL $=$ 58\textsuperscript{\textit{p}},32. Quod ergo « 1\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} $\qfrac{1}{2}$ circiter » \Name{al–Battānī} dicit erat exacte 1\textsuperscript{\textit{p}},96}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L019}{}{}{et 1\textsuperscript{\textit{p}},68; manifeste calculum adproximate instituit.}
\par\vspace{0.12mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L020}{}{}{\Indent « \textit{Inter primam ac alteram latitudinem 0° 3′ 50″ intercedebant} ». —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L021}{}{}{Iuxta numeros Albatenianos prima latitudo (III ecl.) « 0° 32′ circiter in meri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L022}{}{}{« diem versus fuit »; altera (IV ecl.) « 0° 28′ circiter » (plagam indicare omi-}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L023}{}{}{sit, sed ex motu latitudinis 185° 21′ patet australem quoque fuisse). Differentia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L024}{}{}{ergo 4′ pro 3′ 50″. Sed supputatio exactior a me instituta, pro duabus illis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L025}{}{}{latitudinibus 0° 31′ 52″ et 0° 28′ 3″ praebuit; differentia 3′ 49″, bene cum Al-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L026}{}{}{batenianis 3′ 50″ congruens. Manifeste \Name{al–Battānī} eclipsium calculos praecisius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L027}{}{}{duxerat; postea in libro numeros rotundos reddidit. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{-1.37mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-H28}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L028}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{m}}) — \textit{Pag. 58, l. 4–5.}}}
\par\vspace{2.99mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L029}{}{}{\Indent « \textit{Diametro lunari differentia duarum eclipsium} 8 $\qfrac{3}{4}$ \textit{vicibus continetur} ». — Cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L030}{}{}{observationibus auctoris non concordat. In III eclipsi pars obscurata $\qfrac{5}{6}$ diametri fuit; in IV}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L031}{}{}{Luna fere tota. Differentia 1 $-$ $\qfrac{5}{6}$ $=$ $\qfrac{1}{6}$. Ergo pro 8 $\qfrac{3}{4}$ debebat 6 scribere.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent [Puto \Name{al–Battānī}, ne ad absurdam diametrum lunarem vix 23 minuta attingentem (ut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L033}{}{}{e factore 6 necessarie oriebatur) duceretur, quodam modo ad numericam rationem revocare}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L034}{}{}{voluisse illa « paululo amplius $\qfrac{5}{6}$ » tertiae eclipsis, et « perpaulo minus quam sua diametro »}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L035}{}{}{quartae. Cum vero haec numeris indicare difficile esset, factorem 8 $\qfrac{3}{4}$ delegisse, diametrum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L036}{}{}{lunarem 33′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$ aestimando. Solummodo Albatenius (parum perspicue, ut videtur) ostendere vo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L037}{}{}{luit, Lunae diametrum a se propositam cum his duabus eclipsibus satis posse conciliari.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L038}{}{}{\Indent Ergo quae \Name{al–Battānī} dicit non debent vera diametri lunaris determinatio censeri, sed}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L039}{}{}{probatio tantum non repugnare observationes contra diametrum quam receperat. — \textsc{Schia-}}
\par\vspace{0.16mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L040}{}{}{\textsc{parelli}.]}
\par\vspace{-0.48mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-H41}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L041}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{n}}) — \textit{Pag. 58, l. 9–11.}}}
\par\vspace{2.34mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0323-P08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L042}{}{}{\Indent [« \textit{Lunae motus horarius temporibus syzygiarum} ..... ». — Hi motus horarii, maxi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0323-body-L043}{}{}{mus 36′ 10″ et minimus 30′ 12″ (Plato 15″, nam saepissime apud eum 2 fit 5), cum tabula}
\DSource{0324}{235}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L001}{}{}{t. II, p. 88 non conveniunt, e qua 36′ 4″ et 30′ 18″ deducuntur. Causam differentiae nescio;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L002}{}{}{fortasse supputatio Albateniana adproximate tantum ducta est. — In sequentibus calculis po-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L003}{}{}{nit maximam diametrum esse ad minimam ut maximus motus horarius ad minimum. Verum}
\par\vspace{0.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L004}{}{}{esset si motus horarius \textit{anomaliae} aequaretur motui \textit{longitudinis}, ut in Sole evenit; error}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L005}{}{}{tamen etiam in Lunae diametro videtur negligendus. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{8.13mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0324-H06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L006}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{o}}) — \textit{Pag. 58, l. 16–18.}}}
\par\vspace{6.42mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0324-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L007}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Semidiametrum umbrae}, cum Luna apogea sit, 0° 38′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$ fere statuit; cum Luna peri-}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L008}{}{}{gea exstet, 0° 46′ circiter; differentia ergo 0° 7′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$ circiter.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0324-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L009}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Ibn Yūnus} (introd., \Name{Caussin} p. 70–72) haec observat: « Errant quoque Abū ’l–‘Abbās}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L010}{}{}{« al–Faḍl ibn Ḥātim \Name{an–Nayrīzī} (1) et Muḥammad ibn Ǵābir \Name{al–Battānī} cum ponunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L011}{}{}{« semidiametrum umbrae inter maximam et minimam [Lunae] distantiam variare 7′ 12″. Qui-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L012}{}{}{« cumque minus quam duo viri illi in studiis geometriae versatus agnoscet eam sententiam}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L013}{}{}{« falsam esse, si consideraverit demonstrationem \Name{Ptolemaei} [\textit{Almag.} V, 15] de distantia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L014}{}{}{« Solis a centro Terrae. Impossibile est variationem semidiametri umbrae inter distantiam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L015}{}{}{« apogeam Lunae et distantiam perigeam tanti esse quanti illi faciunt, neque 8′ vel 9′ minu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L016}{}{}{« torum; multo maior evadere debet si in hoc statuendo viam demonstrationis geometricae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L017}{}{}{« sequeris. Olim calculum ego institueram, et inveneram 0° 10′ 17″ ponens Solem et Lunam (2)}
\par\vspace{0.13mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L018}{}{}{« in maxima distantia a Terra. Variatio quae semidiametrum umbrae afficit ob proximitatem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L019}{}{}{« Solis Terrae, non potest 1 minutum excedere » (3).}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{143.41mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0324-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L020}{}{}{\Indent [Quae \Name{Ibn Yūnus} dicit hoc modo demonstrantur. Sit RN radius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L021}{}{}{umbrae in minima distantia, quem angulus RMN in minutis et secundis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L022}{}{}{metitur; R′N′ radius umbrae in maxima distantia, eiusque mensura an-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L023}{}{}{gulus R′MN′. Est KRM maxima Lunae parallaxis, iuxta al–Battānī in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L024}{}{}{syzygiis 64′ 51″ complectens; KR′M minima parallaxis, iuxta al–Bat-}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L025}{}{}{tānī 53′ 34″. Sit $\varphi$ angulus KVM in vertice coni umbrae. In triangulo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L026}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.75mm}\smash{\rlap{MRV sunt}}}\rlap{\hspace*{41.11mm}\smash{\rlap{$KRM$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{56.97mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{60.21mm}\smash{\rlap{64′ 51″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{78.45mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{83.23mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$\varphi$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{88.00mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$+$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{91.64mm}\smash{\rlap{$RMV$}}}}
\par\vspace{0.17mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L027}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{41.78mm}\smash{\rlap{$KR'M$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{57.56mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{60.65mm}\smash{\rlap{53′ 34″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{79.03mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{83.61mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$\varphi$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{88.31mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$+$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{92.00mm}\smash{\rlap{$R'MV$}}}}
\par\vspace{1.66mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L028}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{37.52mm}\smash{\rlap{differentia}}}\rlap{\hspace*{61.45mm}\smash{\rlap{$\overline{\text{10′ 17″}}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{79.56mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{83.12mm}\smash{\rlap{$RMV$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{97.59mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$-$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{101.28mm}\smash{\rlap{$R'MV$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{116.93mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{120.64mm}\smash{\rlap{$RMR'$,}}}}
\par\vspace{3.13mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L029}{}{}{qui RMR′ est, in angulo, differentia inter maximam et minimam um-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L030}{}{}{bram. Manifeste angulus 10′ 17″ semper idem est, licet distantia Solis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L031}{}{}{et angulus coni umbrae varient. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0324-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent Numeros suos manifeste \Name{al–Battānī} hoc modo invenit. Putavit}
\par\vspace{-0.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L033}{}{}{(cfr. p. 58, l. 7–8) rationem diametri umbrae ad diametrum Lunae esse}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L034}{}{}{quam \Name{Ptolemaeus}, \textit{Almag.} V, 14 (ed. Halma t. I, p. 343) habet, sci-}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{42.59mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0324-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0324-F01}\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.37mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L035}{}{}{licet ut 2 $\qfrac{3}{5}$ ad 1; hac ratione e diametris Albatenianis maxima et minima Lunae (35′ $\qfrac{1}{3}$ et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L036}{}{}{29′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$) deducuntur diametri umbrae 91′,858 et 76′,7, semidiametri itaque 45′ 54″ et 38′ 42″.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L037}{}{}{Differentia 7′ 12″, quam \Name{Ibn Yūnus} memorat; \Name{al–Battānī} 7′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$ fere scribit, quoniam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-body-L038}{}{}{semidiametros rotunde 46′ et 38′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$ ponit.}
\par\vspace{2.22mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{0.99mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0324-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Anaritius apud Latinos aevi medii scripto-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-notes_left-L002}{}{}{res. Obiit anno circiter 922 Chr.; \Name{Suter}, \textit{Die Ma-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\textit{thematiker und Astronomen}, p. 45, nr. 88.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{1.12mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0324-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (2) [Melius: Solem apogeum, Lunam in apogeo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-notes_right-L002}{}{}{excentrici. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{-0.87mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0324-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0324-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (3) Al-Battānī 0′ 50″ circiter.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0325}{236}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 4.52mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0325-H01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L001}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{p}}) — \textit{Pag. 58, l. 27–31.}}}
\par\vspace{5.16mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0325-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L002}{}{}{\Indent Ex observationibus eclipsium \Name{al–Battānī} \textit{diametros angulares Lunae} et \textit{Solis} melius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L003}{}{}{quam \Name{Ptolemaeus} determinavit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0325-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L004}{}{}{\Indent Diametrus \textit{Solis} 31′ 20″ semper apud Graecum auctorem erat, qui eam variare ignorabat.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L005}{}{}{Noster variationem diametri agnovit, et pro diversis distantiis a Terra invenit:}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L006}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{14.73mm}\smash{\rlap{31′ 20″ in maxima dist.,}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.86mm}\smash{\rlap{32′ 30″ in media,}}}\rlap{\hspace*{101.56mm}\smash{\rlap{33′ 40″ in minima.}}}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0325-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L007}{}{}{\Indent Astronomi aevi nostri (1) statuerunt:}
\par\vspace{1.21mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L008}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{14.42mm}\smash{\rlap{31′ 31″,98 in maxima dist.,}}}\rlap{\hspace*{71.52mm}\smash{\rlap{32′ 3″,64 in media,}}}\rlap{\hspace*{112.31mm}\smash{\rlap{32′ 36″,48 in minima.}}}}
\par\vspace{1.06mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0325-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L009}{}{}{\Indent Diametrus \textit{Lunae} iuxta}
\par\vspace{1.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L010}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.40mm}\smash{\rlap{\Name{Ptolemaeum}:}}}\rlap{\hspace*{32.51mm}\smash{\rlap{31′ 20″ in maxima dist.,}}}\rlap{\hspace*{83.64mm}\smash{\rlap{33′ 20″ in media,}}}\rlap{\hspace*{120.89mm}\smash{\rlap{35′ 20″ in minima;}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L011}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.18mm}\smash{\rlap{\Name{al–Battānī}:}}}\rlap{\hspace*{33.26mm}\smash{\rlap{29′ 30″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{84.62mm}\smash{\rlap{32′ 25″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{121.46mm}\smash{\rlap{35′ 20″}}}}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L012}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.35mm}\smash{\rlap{nostrates (2):}}}\rlap{\hspace*{33.13mm}\smash{\rlap{29′ 26″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{84.35mm}\smash{\rlap{31′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{93.95mm}\smash{\rlap{8″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{121.42mm}\smash{\rlap{33′ 34″}}}}
\par\vspace{1.43mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0325-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L013}{}{}{\Indent Res haud parvi momenti est, nam ex suis mensuris \Name{al–Battānī} (ut iam \Name{Delambre} p. 39}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L014}{}{}{animadvertit) in l. 29–30 deducit \textit{eclipses annulares Solis interdum evenire}; quae contra}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L015}{}{}{fieri non potuissent iuxta mensuras \Name{Ptolemaei}, diametrum Solis aequalem diametro Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L016}{}{}{apogeae statuentes; cfr. \Name{Delambre}, \textit{Histoire de l’astronomie ancienne}, Paris 1817, t. II,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L017}{}{}{p. 234–235, et P. \Name{Tannery}, \textit{Recherches sur l’astronomie ancienne}, Paris 1893, p. 224 et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L018}{}{}{233. Inter Graecos tamen, I saec. ante Chr. n., \Name{Sosigenes} philosophus Peripateticus variatio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L019}{}{}{nem diametri solaris iam cognoverat, et, Proclo Diadocho teste (3), dixerat Solem in eclipsibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L020}{}{}{perigeis « non apparere totum occultatum, sed limbo suae circumferentiae discum lunarem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L021}{}{}{« excedere et sine impedimento lucem praebere » \textgreek{ὁρᾶσθαι μὴ ὅλον ἐπιπροσθούμενον, ἀλλὰ τοῖς}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L022}{}{}{\textgreek{ἄκροις τῆς ἑαυτοῦ περιφερείας ὑπερβάλλειν τὸν κύκλον τῆς σελήνης καὶ φωτίζειν οὐκ ἐμποδιζόμενον}.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L023}{}{}{\Name{Proclus} hanc sententiam falsam demonstrare vult per mensuras Ptolemaicas, dicens si ea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L024}{}{}{vera esset consequi diametrum apparentem Lunae in punctis apogeis differre a diametro Solis;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L025}{}{}{hoc autem doctrinae Ptolemaei contrarium esse.}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0325-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L026}{}{}{\Indent Arabes post \Name{al–Battānī} eclipses annulares Solis in dubium non vocaverunt; anno 1279}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L027}{}{}{Chr., in astronomia Syriace scripta, \Name{Barhebraeus} p. 88 text. narrat: « Diametrum Solis}
\par\vspace{0.11mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L028}{}{}{« ab apogeo ad perigeum invenerunt variare a 31′ ad 34′ (4); diametrum Lunae a 29′ ad 36′.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L029}{}{}{« Cum ambae diametri apparentes aequales sint, Sol totus deficit absque eo quod umbra ex-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L030}{}{}{« cedens reperiatur; cum diametrus Solis maior sit lunari, non solum nulla umbra excedens}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L031}{}{}{« habetur, sed in Sole corona luminosa circa Lunam quae eum occultat cernitur ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0325-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent Etiam XVII Christi saeculo ineunte dubitabant Europaei quidam eclipsem totalem Solis}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L033}{}{}{fieri posse; cfr. G. \Name{Schiaparelli}, \textit{Le sfere omocentriche di Eudosso, di Callippo e di}}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L034}{}{}{\textit{Aristotele}, Milano 1875, pag. 62, adn. 31 ($=$ Pubblicazioni del R. Osservatorio di Brera, Nr. IX).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0325-P08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-body-L035}{}{}{\Indent [Ipsa prima eclipsi 8 Augusti 891 fortasse potuit \Name{al–Battānī} in sententia sua interdum}
\par\vspace{1.27mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{0.92mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0325-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Eos sequor qui in specula Parisiensi cele-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-notes_left-L002}{}{}{berrimas ephemerides \textit{La Connaissance des Temps}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-notes_left-L003}{}{}{et \textit{Annuaire du Bureau des Longitudes} edunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0325-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (2) Iuxta \Name{Babylonios} IV et III saec. ante}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-notes_left-L005}{}{}{Chr. 29′ 26″,9, 31′ 51″,5, 34′ 16″,2, quae sunt melio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-notes_left-L006}{}{}{res etiam quantitatibus Ptolemaicis et Albatenianis.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-notes_left-L007}{}{}{Cfr. \Name{Kugler}, \textit{Die babylonische Mondrechnung},}
\par\vspace{-0.14mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-notes_left-L008}{}{}{Freiburg i. Br. 1900, p. 126.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{0.92mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0325-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (3) \textit{Hypothèses et époques des planètes de}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-notes_right-L002}{}{}{C. \Name{Ptolémée}, \textit{et Hypotyposes de} \Name{Proclus Dia-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Name{dochus} \textit{publiées et traduites par} \Name{Halma}, Pa-}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-notes_right-L004}{}{}{ris 1820, p. 111. — Cfr. quoque \Name{Delambre}, \textit{Hist.}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\textit{de l’astr. anc.}, t. I, p. 314.}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0325-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\Indent (4) Barhebraeus numeris rotundis semper fere}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-notes_right-L007}{}{}{utitur; si gradibus data quantitas constat, minuta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0325-notes_right-L008}{}{}{omittit; si minutis, secundas.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0326}{237}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L001}{}{}{eclipses \textit{annulares} fieri confirmari; nam iuxta \textit{Canonem} \Name{Oppolzer} annularis fuit in linea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L002}{}{}{obliqua Asiam Minorem, Ciliciam et Mesopotamiam transeunte. Ei in ar–Raqqah observanti}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L003}{}{}{poterat nuntium pervenisse mirabilis spectaculi quod locis haud multum remotis apparuerat.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L004}{}{}{— Aliae duae eclipses annulares parum longe ab ar–Raqqah evenerunt 26 Aprilis 906 et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L005}{}{}{5 Aprilis 916, ut apparet e \textit{Canone} \Name{Oppolzer}, nr. 101 tabulae geographicae.— \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{10.42mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0326-H06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L006}{}{}{\CenterLine{A\textsc{d} \textsc{caput} XXXI.}}
\par\vspace{4.56mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0326-H07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L007}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 64, l. 11 et seqq.}}}
\par\vspace{4.40mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0326-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L008}{}{}{\Indent [\textit{Sphaerae vel circuli planetarum.} — Figura pessime descripta et demonstratio ab im-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L009}{}{}{perita manu vitiata. Circulus ABCD concentricus Terrae et in plano eclipticae situs, non potest}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L010}{}{}{in eodem iacere plano quo circulus GBFD concentricus quidem Terrae sed parvo angulo in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L011}{}{}{clinatus respectu eclipticae. Ergo si GBFD cum reliqua figura describatur in plano folii, cir-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L012}{}{}{culus ABCD, extra folium incidens, non poterit circulo cuius centrum E repraesentari. —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L013}{}{}{Ponit quoque figura nodos B, D horum circulorum 90 gradibus distare a linea GK, absides}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L014}{}{}{excentrici determinante; quod in nullo planeta contingit, nec poterat \Name{al–Battānī} verum vi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L015}{}{}{deri. — Rectae ETN, EXZ non debent esse epicyclo tangentes, nam planeta quemlibet epicycli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L016}{}{}{locum occupare censendus est. Ipsae autem in \textit{ecliptica} non sunt, sed in plano \textit{deflectentis}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L017}{}{}{GBFD et excentrici GHKL; quod tamen solummodo verum est cum \textit{false} epicyclus putetur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L018}{}{}{in plano excentrici iacere.}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{90.90mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0326-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L019}{}{}{\Indent Figuram restaurare conatus sum quam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L020}{}{}{probabiliter \Name{al–Battānī} voluerat; quae, ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L021}{}{}{fidem codicis et Platonis, p. 64 impressa est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L022}{}{}{vitio verti debet imperito scriptori archetypi,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L023}{}{}{qui, ut iam alibi vidimus, ad figuram male}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L024}{}{}{descriptam textum postea accommodare stu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L025}{}{}{duit. Punctum A repraesentavit quasi esset in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L026}{}{}{plano GBFD; falso quoque imaginatus est M,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L027}{}{}{excentrici centrum, esse punctum circa quod}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L028}{}{}{motus uniformis centri epicycli fit, cum contra}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L029}{}{}{hic motus circa punctum E′ eveniat, quod igi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L030}{}{}{tur centrum est circuli a Ptolemaei asseclis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L031}{}{}{\textit{aequantis} vocati (1). Punctum E′ ita est ut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L032}{}{}{sit ME′$=$ME; cfr. \Name{Delambre}, \textit{Hist. de l’astr.}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L033}{}{}{\textit{anc.}, t. II, p. 316.}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0326-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L034}{}{}{\Indent Non improbabile censeo in vera Albatenii}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{95.10mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0326-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0326-F01}\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.34mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L035}{}{}{figura exstitisse, circa centrum E′ descriptum, circulum \textit{aequantem} (in quo epicycli centrum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L036}{}{}{temporibus aequalibus aequalia angula percurrit); postea a lectore quodam in circulum con-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L037}{}{}{centricum Terrae et eclipticae conversum esse, unde omnia perturbata evasisse. Tunc perspi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L038}{}{}{cuum foret, cur talis circuli centrum in linea GF, et cur nodos in recta perpendiculari ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-body-L039}{}{}{GF, scilicet in B et D locaverit.}
\par\vspace{1.37mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{0.74mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0326-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Apud Arabes, ut \Name{al-Farghānī} cap. XIV,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Name{al-Ǵaghmīnī} 249-250, cet., \textit{al-falak al-mu-}}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{0.74mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\textit{maththil li ’l-masīr} « sphaera (vel circulus) exae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0326-notes_right-L002}{}{}{« quans motum »,}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0327}{238}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 0.08mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0327-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent Demonstratio tota falsa est in hoc quod, contra hypothesim Ptolemaei, ponit M centrum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L002}{}{}{motus uniformis epicycli H in excentrico; pro M erat E′ considerandum. Parvae aliae corre-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L003}{}{}{ctiones iam suis locis p. 64–65 indicatae sunt, quibus fortasse vera \Name{al–Battānī} demonstratio}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L004}{}{}{restituitur. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{7.76mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0327-H05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L005}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{b}}) — \textit{Pag. 65, l. 17–23.}}}
\par\vspace{6.71mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0327-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L006}{}{}{\Indent [« \textit{Regressuum planetarum causa est.....} ». — Data die sit $\Delta l$ motus planetae in lon-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L007}{}{}{gitudine in ecliptica, $\Delta m$ motus medius diurnus, $\Delta\text{\textgreek{ε}}$ variatio aequationis; \Name{al–Battānī} ait,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L008}{}{}{quoniam $\Delta l = \Delta m + \Delta\text{\textgreek{ε}}$, si $\Delta\text{\textgreek{ε}}$ negativa et maior quam $\Delta m$ sit, $\Delta l$ quoque negativam esse}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L009}{}{}{et planetam retrogradum. Hoc sensus linearum 17–19. — Quae postea leguntur; « si locus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L010}{}{}{« verus planetae » usque ad l. 24, sunt obscurissima repetitio eiusdem rei; sed quo modo lo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L011}{}{}{cus sanandus nescio. Sit $l$ longitudo vera hodie, $l + \Delta l$ cras, $m$ motus medius hodie, $m + \Delta m$}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L012}{}{}{cras, $\text{\textgreek{ε}}$ aequatio hodierna, $\text{\textgreek{ε}} + \Delta\text{\textgreek{ε}}$ crastina; procul dubio erit}
\par\vspace{4.57mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L013}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{28.40mm}\smash{\rlap{$l = m + \text{\textgreek{ε}}$,}}}\rlap{\hspace*{60.40mm}\smash{\rlap{$\Delta l = \Delta m + \Delta\text{\textgreek{ε}}$,}}}\rlap{\hspace*{101.68mm}\smash{\rlap{$l + \Delta l = m + \Delta m + \text{\textgreek{ε}} + \Delta\text{\textgreek{ε}}$.}}}}
\par\vspace{5.26mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0327-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L014}{}{}{\Indent Qualis harum aequalitatum in textu indicetur non intelligo. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{7.96mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0327-H15}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L015}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{c}}) — \textit{Pag. 65, l. 23–34.}}}
\par\vspace{6.63mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0327-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L016}{}{}{\Indent Iure \Name{Delambre} p. 40 scribit: « Les rétrogradations arrivent lorsque le mouvement sur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L017}{}{}{« l’épicycle est égal ou contraire au mouvement du centre de l’épicycle; ce qui a lieu, dit–il}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L018}{}{}{« [i. e. \Name{al–Battānī}], quand la planète est dans le rayon visuel tangent à l’épicycle, ce qui}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L019}{}{}{« n’est pas exact, car alors le mouvement dans l’épicycle est nul, et il reste le mouvement}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L020}{}{}{« du centre sans aucune compensation (1); c’est une inadvertance ». — Idem \Name{Delambre} p. 99,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L021}{}{}{ubi de IX cap. \Name{Ibn Yūnus} loquitur, haec habet: « Il énonce, comme Albatégni, que la ré-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L022}{}{}{« trogradation commence lorsque la planète se meut suivant la tangente à l’orbite. Si ce n’est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L023}{}{}{« pas une erreur positive, c’est au moins une expression bien impropre. Ptolémée s’était ex-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L024}{}{}{« primé avec beaucoup plus de justesse ».}
\par\vspace{0.11mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0327-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L025}{}{}{\Indent Patet ex hoc \Name{al–Battānī} et \Name{Ibn Yūnus} adproximate tantum loca regressuum plane-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L026}{}{}{tarum indicare voluisse. — Sed alius quoque error apud nostrum invenitur, cum ponit puncta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L027}{}{}{contactus tangentium (X et T) esse eadem loca in quibus epicyclus excentricum secat; ex}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L028}{}{}{ipsa codicis et Platonis figura (pag. 64) id impossibile apparet.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0327-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L029}{}{}{\Indent Procul dubio multa huius capitis quae ad explicandam theoriam planetarum inserviunt}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L030}{}{}{ab \Name{al–Battānī} non proveniunt. Auctor hoc caput praesertim scripsit ut declararet qua ra-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L031}{}{}{tione tabulas planetarum Ptolemaicas correxisset (p. 65–66); elementa cosmographica lectoribus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L032}{}{}{suis iam nota censebat (ut in reliquis libri partibus), qua re summatim tantum fortasse sphae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L033}{}{}{ras vel circulos planetarum memoraverat, auxilio figurae. Brevem theoriae expositionem, ut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L034}{}{}{puto, solitus indoctus lector pro parvis suis viribus conatus est uberiorem reddere, et hoc}
\par\vspace{-0.18mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-body-L035}{}{}{modo textum Albatenianum deturpavit.}
\par\vspace{4.05mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}{\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.10mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0327-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0327-notes-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) I. e. eo loco planeta motum epicycli in excentrico participat.}
}
\DSource{0328}{239}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 5.23mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0328-H01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L001}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{d}}) — \textit{Pag. 66, l. 1–8, 12–15.}}}
\par\vspace{4.45mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0328-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L002}{}{}{\Indent I. — \textit{Motus longitudinis planetarum superiorum} dicit (l. 1–2) se maiores quam quos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L003}{}{}{Ptolemaeus statuerat invenisse. Re vera e tabulis ita motus medii in intervallo 20 annorum}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L004}{}{}{Iulianorum (1), vel 20 Aegyptiorum (sine intercalatione) $+$ 5 dierum, colliguntur:}
\par\vspace{3.61mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L005}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{69.56mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{{\fontsize{9.5}{11}\selectfont \textbf{Saturnus}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{111.97mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{{\fontsize{9.5}{11}\selectfont \textbf{Iupiter}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{154.25mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{{\fontsize{9.5}{11}\selectfont \textbf{Mars}}}}}}
\par\nointerlineskip\vspace{1pt}\noindent\rlap{\hspace*{52.29mm}\smash{\raisebox{-5.5pt}{$\overbrace{\hspace{33.87mm}}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{94.88mm}\smash{\raisebox{-5.5pt}{$\overbrace{\hspace{33.74mm}}$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{137.38mm}\smash{\raisebox{-5.5pt}{$\overbrace{\hspace{33.74mm}}$}}}\par\nointerlineskip\vspace{4pt}\prevdepth=\dp\strutbox
\par\vspace{-0.18mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L006}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{17.80mm}\smash{\rlap{\Name{al–Battānī}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{59.02mm}\smash{\llap{244}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{66.54mm}\smash{\llap{42}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{73.32mm}\smash{\llap{44}\rlap{″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{81.91mm}\smash{\llap{20}\rlap{‴}}}\rlap{\hspace*{101.88mm}\smash{\llap{247}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{109.49mm}\smash{\llap{17}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{116.58mm}\smash{\llap{34}\rlap{″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{124.85mm}\smash{\llap{26}\rlap{‴}}}\rlap{\hspace*{144.82mm}\smash{\llap{228}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{152.44mm}\smash{\llap{22}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{159.43mm}\smash{\llap{10}\rlap{″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{167.49mm}\smash{\llap{49}\rlap{‴}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L007}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{17.89mm}\smash{\rlap{\Name{Ptolemaeus}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{59.02mm}\smash{\llap{244}}}\rlap{\hspace*{66.54mm}\smash{\llap{38}}}\rlap{\hspace*{73.32mm}\smash{\llap{1}}}\rlap{\hspace*{81.91mm}\smash{\llap{38}}}\rlap{\hspace*{101.88mm}\smash{\llap{247}}}\rlap{\hspace*{109.49mm}\smash{\llap{12}}}\rlap{\hspace*{116.58mm}\smash{\llap{33}}}\rlap{\hspace*{124.85mm}\smash{\llap{49}}}\rlap{\hspace*{144.82mm}\smash{\llap{228}}}\rlap{\hspace*{152.44mm}\smash{\llap{15}}}\rlap{\hspace*{159.43mm}\smash{\llap{22}}}\rlap{\hspace*{167.49mm}\smash{\llap{17}}}}
\par\vspace{0.67mm}
\par\nointerlineskip\vspace{1pt}\noindent\rlap{\hspace*{52.38mm}\rule{33.82mm}{.4pt}}\rlap{\hspace*{94.88mm}\rule{33.74mm}{.4pt}}\rlap{\hspace*{137.30mm}\rule{33.78mm}{.4pt}}\par\nointerlineskip\vspace{2pt}
\par\vspace{0.05mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L008}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{38.25mm}\smash{\rlap{differ.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{59.02mm}\smash{\llap{0}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{66.54mm}\smash{\llap{4}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{73.32mm}\smash{\llap{42}\rlap{″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{81.91mm}\smash{\llap{42}\rlap{‴}}}\rlap{\hspace*{101.88mm}\smash{\llap{0}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{109.49mm}\smash{\llap{5}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{116.58mm}\smash{\llap{0}\rlap{″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{124.85mm}\smash{\llap{37}\rlap{‴}}}\rlap{\hspace*{144.82mm}\smash{\llap{0}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{152.44mm}\smash{\llap{6}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{159.43mm}\smash{\llap{48}\rlap{″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{167.49mm}\smash{\llap{32}\rlap{‴}}}}
\par\vspace{3.69mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0328-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L009}{}{}{\Indent Itaque in 740 circiter annis Iulianis intercedentibus inter epocham tabularum Ptolemaei}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L010}{}{}{(137 Chr.) et epocham tabularum al–Battānī (880 Chr.) differentiae fuerunt {\MoonFont ♄} 2° 54′ 19″ 54‴,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L011}{}{}{{\MoonFont ♃} 3° 5′ 22″ 49‴, {\MoonFont ♂} 4° 11′ 55″ 45‴.}
\par\vspace{2.63mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L012}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{1.33mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{Tabula Probata}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{39.94mm}\smash{\rlap{\smash{\raisebox{-16.6pt}{\tikz\draw[line width=.55pt,decorate,decoration={brace,amplitude=2.3pt}] (0,25.8pt) -- (0,0);}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{53.13mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\llap{244}\rlap{°}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{60.88mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\llap{43}\rlap{′}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{67.96mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\llap{14}\rlap{″}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{86.42mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\llap{247}\rlap{°}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{94.26mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\llap{17}\rlap{′}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{101.03mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\llap{46}\rlap{″}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{109.27mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\llap{20}\rlap{‴}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{129.64mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\llap{228}\rlap{°}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{137.16mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\llap{23}\rlap{′}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{144.07mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\llap{2}\rlap{″}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{152.44mm}\smash{\raisebox{-.55\baselineskip}{\llap{20}\rlap{‴}}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L013}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{1.06mm}\smash{\rlap{\Name{Ḥabash} (2)}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L014}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.93mm}\smash{\rlap{Astron. hodierni}}}\rlap{\hspace*{53.13mm}\smash{\llap{244}}}\rlap{\hspace*{60.88mm}\smash{\llap{42}}}\rlap{\hspace*{67.96mm}\smash{\llap{5}}}\rlap{\hspace*{86.42mm}\smash{\llap{247}}}\rlap{\hspace*{94.26mm}\smash{\llap{15}}}\rlap{\hspace*{101.03mm}\smash{\llap{31}}}\rlap{\hspace*{129.64mm}\smash{\llap{228}}}\rlap{\hspace*{137.16mm}\smash{\llap{20}}}\rlap{\hspace*{144.07mm}\smash{\llap{14}}}}
\par\vspace{2.14mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0328-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L015}{}{}{\Indent Patet numeros meliores Ptolemaicis \Name{al-Battānī} invenisse, et procul fere dubio obser-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L016}{}{}{vationibus quoque astronomorum khalīfae al–Ma’mūn usum esse.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0328-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L017}{}{}{\Indent II. — \textit{Apogea planetarum} narrat se invenisse iisdem fere locis in quibus (praecessione}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L018}{}{}{aequinoctiorum in intervallo temporis addita) iuxta tabulas Ptolemaei esse debebant, excepta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L019}{}{}{tamen longitudine apogei Iovis 8° fere minore quam longitudine Ptolemaica.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0328-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L020}{}{}{\Indent Apogeorum longitudines pro anno 1191 Dhū ’l–qarnayn (inc. 1 Sept. 879 Chr.) indicantur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L021}{}{}{in fine XLV capitis (p. 114) et in titulis tabularum aequationum planetae; a \Name{Ptolemaeo},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L022}{}{}{\textit{Almag.} IX, 3 (ed. Halma t. II, p. 128 seqq.), pro anno 137. Sed ante quam eas inter se com-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L023}{}{}{paremus, necesse est ut inquiramus an numeri codicis recte scripti sint.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0328-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Al–Bīrūnī}, in libello inedito de elementis astronomiae, f. 28,r. et v., scribit: « Apogeo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L025}{}{}{« rum loca hoc nostro tempore, anno scilicet 420 hegirae [inc. 19 Ian. 1029], iuxta quae Mu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L026}{}{}{« ḥammad ibn Ǵābir \Name{al–Battānī} invenit debent esse: apogeum \SunSym{} 24° 32′ Gemin., {\MoonFont ♄} 6° 48′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L027}{}{}{« Sagitt., {\MoonFont ♃} 16° 43′ Virg., {\MoonFont ♂} 8° 30′ Leonis, {\MoonFont ♀} 24° 39′ Gemin., {\MoonFont ☿} 23° 43′ Librae ». In intervallo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L028}{}{}{149 annorum solarium praecessio iuxta al–Battanī et al–Bīrūnī fuit 2° 15′; haec igitur diffe-}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L029}{}{}{rentia in omnibus planetis reperiri debet. Tabella comparationis praebet:}
\par\vspace{3.84mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0328-body-L030}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{19.70mm}\smash{\rlap{\Name{al–Bīrūnī}:}}}\rlap{\hspace*{48.13mm}\smash{\rlap{{\MoonFont ♄}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{73.19mm}\smash{\rlap{{\MoonFont ♃}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{99.31mm}\smash{\rlap{{\MoonFont ♂}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{124.90mm}\smash{\rlap{{\MoonFont ♀}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{147.57mm}\smash{\rlap{{\MoonFont ☿}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{59.86mm}\smash{\llap{246}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{67.34mm}\smash{\llap{48}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{86.29mm}\smash{\llap{166}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{93.77mm}\smash{\llap{43}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{111.39mm}\smash{\llap{128}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{118.88mm}\smash{\llap{30}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{134.24mm}\smash{\llap{84}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{141.55mm}\smash{\llap{39}\rlap{′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{158.95mm}\smash{\llap{203}\rlap{°}}}\rlap{\hspace*{166.78mm}\smash{\llap{43}\rlap{′}}}}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0329-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent In \textit{Libro de las láminas de las VII planetas,} cuius prima pars (16 complectens ca-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L002}{}{}{pita) est versio Hispanica operis Arabici ab \Name{Abulcacim Abnaçahm} i e. Abū ’l–qāsim}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L003}{}{}{Aṣbagh \Name{Ibn as–Samḥ} (1) confecti, parte I, cap. XIII (2), praecessione [Albateniana] unius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L004}{}{}{gradus in 68 annis lunaribus sumpta, apogea planetarum pro anno 416 heg. (inc. 3 Mart. 1025)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L005}{}{}{praebet, quae manifeste ab Albatenianis proveniunt. Numeri ita sunt: {\MoonFont ♄} 246° 40′, {\MoonFont ♃} 166° 40′,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L006}{}{}{{\MoonFont ♂} 129° 10′, \SunSym{} 84° 27′, {\MoonFont ♀} $=$ \SunSym{} (« tal cuemo ell auxe del sol »), {\MoonFont ☿} 203° 40′. Intervallum in-}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L007}{}{}{ter al–Battānī et Ibn as–Samḥ est 145 annorum Iulianorum; praecessio igitur 2° 12′. Haec}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L008}{}{}{quantitas de longitudinibus illis dematur; proveniunt apogea pro epocha tabularum Albate-}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L009}{}{}{nianarum:}
\par\vspace{2.23mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L010}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{18.02mm}\smash{\rlap{{\MoonFont ♄} 244° 28′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{48.09mm}\smash{\rlap{{\MoonFont ♃} 164° 28′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{78.87mm}\smash{\rlap{{\MoonFont ♂} 126° 58′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{109.52mm}\smash{\rlap{\SunSym{} et {\MoonFont ♀} 82° 15′}}}\rlap{\hspace*{150.08mm}\smash{\rlap{{\MoonFont ☿} 201° 28′,}}}}
\par\vspace{2.50mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L011}{}{}{quae cum numeris al–Battānī omnino congruunt, excepto apogeo Martis ubi minuta codicis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L012}{}{}{Escurialensis \ArabicRun{يح} 18′ in \ArabicRun{نح} 58′ mutanda videntur (3).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0329-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L013}{}{}{\Indent In secunda parte eiusdem libri \textit{de las láminas de las VII planetas,} quae est versio opu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L014}{}{}{sculi astronomi Arabici \Name{Abuizac el Zarquiel} i. e. Abū Isḥāq \Name{az–Zarqālī}, cap. II (\textit{Libros}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L015}{}{}{\textit{del saber de astronomia,} t. III, p. 272–273), auctor dicit apogea el \Name{Bateni} (i. e. Albatenii)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L016}{}{}{se ad annum hegirae 473 (vel 468?) reduxisse; sed numeri Romani tantis mendis scatent ut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L017}{}{}{nullo modo restitui possint.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0329-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L018}{}{}{\Indent Auxilium quoque nobis \Name{al-Ǵaghmīnī} p. 253 praebet, qui praecessionem Albatenianam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L019}{}{}{sumit. Eius apogea haec sunt:}
\par\vspace{2.35mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L020}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{3.81mm}\smash{\rlap{\SunSym{} 87° 10′ 33″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{41.85mm}\smash{\rlap{{\MoonFont ♄} 249° 23′ 33″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{80.24mm}\smash{\rlap{{\MoonFont ♃} 169° 23′ 33″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{119.44mm}\smash{\rlap{{\MoonFont ♂} 131° 53′ 46″}}}\rlap{\hspace*{158.41mm}\smash{\rlap{{\MoonFont ♀} 87° 10′ 33″}}}}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L021}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{76.08mm}\smash{\rlap{{\MoonFont ☿} 206° 23′ 33″ (4).}}}}
\par\vspace{2.65mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0329-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L022}{}{}{\Indent Omnia exacte ab Albatenianis differunt quantitate 4° 55′ 33″, \textit{Marte} excepto ubi differentia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L023}{}{}{5° 35′ 46″. At si minuta Albateniana \ArabicRun{يح} 18′ in \ArabicRun{نح} 58′ corrigantur ut \Name{Ibn as–Samḥ}, \Name{al–Kha-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L024}{}{}{\Name{raqī}, et \Name{Ptolemaeus} quoque postulant, differentia rursus fit 4° 55′ 46″, secundis tantum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L025}{}{}{discrepantibus. — Differentia praecessionalis 4° 55′ 33″ respondet intervallo 325 annorum Iu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L026}{}{}{lianorum et 30 dierum; apogea al–Battānī sunt pro anno 1191 Dhū ’l–qarnayn, iuxta Alba-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L027}{}{}{tenianum usum die 1 \textit{Sept.} 879 Chr. incipiente; ergo \Name{al–Ǵaghmīnī} ea ad annum 1516 re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L028}{}{}{duxit, iuxta communem usum die 1 \textit{Octobris} 1204 Chr. incipientis (5).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0329-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L029}{}{}{\Indent De apogeo \textit{Veneris}, quod \Name{al–Battānī} aequale apogeo Solis ponit, et cuius minuta in-}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0329-body-L030}{}{}{certa sunt inter 14′ et 15′, cfr. adn. \textbf{\textit{c}} ad XXVIII cap. (pag. 214).}

\par\vspace{1.85mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0330-body-L005}{}{}{Ergo re vera discrepantia 8° graduum in Iove reperitur, quam al–Battānī indicaverat; magna}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0330-body-L006}{}{}{differentia in Venere ex hoc provenit quod al–Battānī, contra Ptolemaei opinionem, eius apo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0330-body-L007}{}{}{geum cum apogeo Solis in longitudine congruere statuit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0330-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0330-body-L008}{}{}{\Indent Differentia tamen 20° 18′ (ob praecessionem) respondet intervallo 1340 annorum Iuliano-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0330-body-L009}{}{}{rum, non 742 aut 743 qui sunt inter epocham Ptolemaei et epocham al–Battānī. In catalogo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0330-body-L010}{}{}{stellarum fixarum (et cfr. quoque LI cap., p. 124) longitudines Ptolemaicas 11° 10′ auxerat;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0330-body-L011}{}{}{cur in apogeis planetarum augmento 20° 18′ usus sit nescio. Manifeste suis observationibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0330-body-L012}{}{}{nisus; sed mirum de his 20° 18′ $-$ 11° 10′ $=$ 9° 8′ praecessioni additis eum locutum non esse.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0330-body-L013}{}{}{Animadvertendum est etiam auctores \textit{Tabulae Probatae}, qui apogea anno 199 Iezdegerdis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0330-body-L014}{}{}{(inc. 28 Apr. 830), 215 hegirae (inc. 27 Febr. 830 Chr.) determinaverunt, longitudines Ptole-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0330-body-L015}{}{}{maicis multo maiores invenisse quam quae e praecessione sola oriri debuissent. Sunt enim (1):}
\par\vspace{2.16mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0330-body-L016}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{66.31mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{{\fontsize{9.5}{11}\selectfont \textbf{Saturnus}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{90.63mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{{\fontsize{9.5}{11}\selectfont \textbf{Iupiter}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{115.63mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{{\fontsize{9.5}{11}\selectfont \textbf{Mars}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.97mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{{\fontsize{9.5}{11}\selectfont \textbf{Venus}}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{165.09mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{{\fontsize{9.5}{11}\selectfont \textbf{Mercurius}}}}}}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0330-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0330-body-L029}{}{}{\Indent De his differentiis iudicium reddere difficile est, nam excentricus hypothesis Ptolemaicae non}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0330-body-L030}{}{}{respondet exacte ellipsi Keplerianae. Motus circularis et uniformis planetae in epicyclo mo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0330-body-L031}{}{}{tum ellipticum et inaequalem Terrae circa Solem proxime tantum, non praecise, repraesentat.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0330-body-L032}{}{}{Antiqui igitur et Arabes, ad observationes recte exprimendas, effectum excentricitatis et inae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0330-body-L033}{}{}{qualitatis motus Terrae transtulerunt in excentricum; unde consequitur excentricitates et apo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0330-body-L034}{}{}{gea Ptolemaei et Arabum nonnisi esse compositiones excentricitatis et apogei planetae cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0330-body-L035}{}{}{excentricitate et apogeo axis terrestris. Si quis iudicare vellet quantum perfectionis observa-}
\par\vspace{0.79mm}

\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0331-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0331-body-L004}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Venerem} quod attinet, cuius apogeum apud al-Battānī et Arabes fere omnes ab apogeo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0331-body-L005}{}{}{Solis non differt, animadvertendum est minimam esse excentricitatem orbitae ellipticae quam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0331-body-L006}{}{}{Venus circa Solem ambit, qua re hanc orbitam considerari posse epicyclum exacte circula-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0331-body-L007}{}{}{rem; tunc deferentem ipsam fieri orbitam Solis, circa quem nos hodie scimus planetam volvi.}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0331-body-L008}{}{}{Licet \Name{al–Battānī} et auctores \textit{Tabulae Probatae} hanc ultimam rem ignorarent, tamen intel-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0331-body-L009}{}{}{lexerunt bene posse motum Veneris repraesentari cum eius \textit{excentrico deferenti} tribuatur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0331-body-L010}{}{}{\textit{eadem excentricitas idemque apogeum} quae orbita Solis habet. Ergo non solum eadem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0331-body-L011}{}{}{apogei longitudo, sed eadem quoque excentricitas et aequatio centri, ut e tabulis Albatenianis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0331-body-L012}{}{}{patet. \Name{Ptolemaeus} contra eadem excentricitate et aequatione centri usus est (\textit{Almag.} III,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0331-body-L013}{}{}{5 et XI, 11, ed. Halma t. I, p. 201, et II, 307), sed apogeum Veneris 20° fere ab apogeo Solis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0331-body-L014}{}{}{distare posuit (1). — Quis rectius senserit iudicari nequit, nisi antea improbo labore bene sta-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0331-body-L015}{}{}{tuatur quo modo excentricus Veneris compositionem orbitarum ellipticarum Terrae et Veneris}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0331-body-L016}{}{}{repraesentet. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L001}{}{}{« driae] conditoris » supputantes (1). Est \textit{aera Philippi Aridaei} (vel Arrhidaei), successoris,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L002}{}{}{ut dicunt, Alexandri Magni, cuius erat frater uterinus; qua re plerumque Arabes eam \textit{aeram}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L003}{}{}{\textit{Philippi} vocant. — Anni huius aerae interdum memorantur in \textit{Almagesto}, ubi de observa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L004}{}{}{tionibus Hipparchi agitur; sunt ibi anni \textit{vagi} (i. e. 365 dierum, sine intercalatione Iuliana) « a}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L005}{}{}{« morte Alexandri » \textgreek{ἀπὸ τῆς Ἀλεξάνδρου τελευτῆς} supputati, et eorum epocha est feria prima}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L006}{}{}{(dies Dominica) 1 thōth 425 Nabonassaris, i. e. 12 Novembris 324 ante Christum natum. Ean-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L007}{}{}{dem epocham, et cum annis \textit{vagis} (seu Persicis seu Aegyptiis), servant \Name{al–Farghānī}, \Name{Ḥa-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L008}{}{}{\Name{bash}, \Name{al–Mas‘ūdī}, \Name{al–Bīrūnī}, \Name{Ibn Yūnus}, \Name{Kūshiyār al–Ǵīlī}, ceterique; qua re inter}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L009}{}{}{aeram Seleucidarum et aeram Philippi intervallum ponunt 4341 dierum, i. e. 11 annorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L010}{}{}{vagorum, 10 mensium et 26 dierum (2). — Sed in \textit{Tabulis Manualibus} \Name{Theonis}, iuxta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L011}{}{}{usum Alexandrinum I saec. Chr. ortum, anni sunt \textit{Iuliani}, et \textit{epocha aerae 29 Augusti 324}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L012}{}{}{ante Chr. natum; \Name{al–Battānī} (cfr. p. 69, l. 20–22) Theonem sequitur, qua re inter aeram}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L013}{}{}{Dhū ’l–qarnayn (a 1 Septembris, non 1 Octobris supputatam) et aeram Alexandri Macedonis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L014}{}{}{intervallum 12 annorum Iulianorum, 3 diebus demptis, ponit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0332-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L015}{}{}{\Indent In opere Persico \textit{Āyīn–i–Akbarī}, quod anno 1594 Chr. \Name{Abū ’l–faẓl ‘Allāmī} compo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L016}{}{}{suit iussu Akbar, Indiae imperatoris, de aera Romana (\textit{ta’rīkh i rūmī}) i. e. Dhū ’l–qarnayn}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L017}{}{}{haec scripta leguntur: « \Name{Al–Battānī} hanc aeram a Philippo patre Alexandri Dhū ’l–qar-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L018}{}{}{« nayn [esse] dicit, sed ab eius filii nomine, celebritatis causa, vocat. In tabulis suis radices}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L019}{}{}{« mediorum motuum ad hanc aeram ponit » (3).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0332-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L020}{}{}{\Indent De \textit{nominibus mensium Coptorum} apud Arabes iam dixi pag. 209, adn. 3; nunc eorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L021}{}{}{conspectum addo:}
\par\vspace{4.43mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L022}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{37.94mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{{\fontsize{9.5}{11}\selectfont Memphitica}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{65.55mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{{\fontsize{9.5}{11}\selectfont Graeca}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{91.70mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{{\fontsize{9.5}{11}\selectfont Arabica (4)}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{118.93mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{{\fontsize{9.5}{11}\selectfont Thebana}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{150.60mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{{\fontsize{9.5}{11}\selectfont Arabica (5)}}}}}
\par\vspace{0.39mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L023}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{23.70mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲑⲱⲟⲩⲧ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{57.69mm}\smash{\rlap{\textgreek{ϑώϑ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{80.55mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{tūt}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.27mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲑⲟⲟⲩⲧ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.26mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{tūt}}}}}

\par\vspace{-0.15mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L025}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{23.70mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲁⲑⲱⲣ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{57.69mm}\smash{\rlap{\textgreek{ἀϑύρ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{80.55mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{atūr}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.27mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ϩⲁⲑⲱⲣ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.26mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{hatūr}}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L026}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{23.70mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲭⲟⲓⲁⲕ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{57.69mm}\smash{\rlap{\textgreek{χοϊάκ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{80.55mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{shuwāq}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.27mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲭⲟⲓⲁϩⲕ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.26mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{kiyahk}}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L027}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{23.70mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲧⲱⲃⲓ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{57.69mm}\smash{\rlap{\textgreek{τυβί}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{80.55mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{ṭūbī}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.27mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲧⲱⲃⲉ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.26mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{ṭūbah}}}}}
\par\vspace{0.26mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L028}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{23.70mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲙⲉⲭⲓⲣ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{57.69mm}\smash{\rlap{\textgreek{μεχίρ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{80.55mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{mākīr}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.27mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲙ̄ϣⲓⲣ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.26mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{amshīr}}}}}
\par\vspace{-0.12mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L029}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{23.70mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲫⲁⲙⲉⲛⲱⲑ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{57.69mm}\smash{\rlap{\textgreek{φαμενώϑ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{80.55mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{fāmīnūth}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.27mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲡⲁⲣⲙϩⲁⲧ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.26mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{barmahāt}}}}}
\par\vspace{-0.12mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L030}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{23.70mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲫⲁⲣⲙⲟⲩⲑⲓ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{57.69mm}\smash{\rlap{\textgreek{φαρμουϑί}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{80.55mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{fārmūthī}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.27mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲡⲁⲣⲙⲟⲩⲧⲉ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.26mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{barmūdhah}}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L031}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{23.70mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲡⲁϣⲱⲛⲥ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{57.69mm}\smash{\rlap{\textgreek{παχώμ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{80.55mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{bākhūn}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.27mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲡⲁϣⲱⲛⲥ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.26mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{bashans}}}}}
\par\vspace{0.15mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L032}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{23.70mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲡⲁⲱⲛⲓ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{57.69mm}\smash{\rlap{\textgreek{παϋνί}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{80.55mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{bāwūnī}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.27mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲡⲁⲱⲛⲉ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.26mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{bawūnah}}}}}
\par\vspace{-0.18mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L033}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{23.70mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲉⲡⲏⲡⲓ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{57.69mm}\smash{\rlap{\textgreek{ἐπιφί}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{80.55mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{efīfī}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.27mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲉⲡⲏⲡ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.26mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{abīb}}}}}
\par\vspace{0.14mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-body-L034}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{23.70mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲙⲉⲥⲱⲣⲏ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{57.69mm}\smash{\rlap{\textgreek{μεσορῆ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{80.55mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{māsūrī}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.27mm}\smash{\rlap{\textcoptic{ⲙⲉⲥⲱⲣⲏ}}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.26mm}\smash{\rlap{\textit{misrī}}}}}

\par\vspace{5.05mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0332-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) \textgreek{Ἀπὸ τοῦ πρώτου ἔτους Φιλίππου τοῦ μετ’ Ἀλέ}-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\textgreek{ξανδρον τὸν κτίστην}; \textit{Commentaires de} \Name{Théon d’A-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Name{léxandrie} \textit{sur les tables manuelles astronomi-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\textit{ques de} \Name{Ptolémée}, publiés et traduits par \Name{Hal-}}
\par\vspace{-0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Name{ma}, Paris 1822, t. I, p. 2 (Ptol.) et 27 (Theon.)}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (2) Apud \Name{al-Mas‘ūdī}, \textit{Tanbīh} 198, perpe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-notes_left-L007}{}{}{ram 12 ann., 10 mens., 20 dierum.}
\par\vspace{-0.11mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0332-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-notes_left-L008}{}{}{\Indent (3) \textit{The Áin i Akbari of} \Name{Abul Fazl i Mu-}}
