\par\vspace{4.96mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Name{bárak i ’Allámí}, \textit{edited by} H. \Name{Blochmann},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-notes_right-L002}{}{}{Calcutta 1867-77, t. I, p. 275 ($=$ \textit{translated by}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-notes_right-L003}{}{}{H. \Name{Blochmann} \textit{and} H. S. \Name{Jarrett}, Calcutta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-notes_right-L004}{}{}{1873-94, t. II, p. 25; sed hoc loco versio minus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-notes_right-L005}{}{}{bona est).}
\par\vspace{-0.18mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0332-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\Indent (4) Cum de annis vagis Nabonassaris agitur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0332-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\Indent (5) Cum ad aeram Nabonassaris non referan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0332-notes_right-L008}{}{}{tur; adhuc in Aegypto vigent.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0333}{244}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 4.44mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0333-H01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L001}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{b}}) — \textit{Pag. 68, l. 23 et seqq.}}}
\par\vspace{5.29mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0333-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L002}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Aera Coptorum.} — Apud reliquos mihi notos Arabicos scriptores hoc nomine tres aerae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L003}{}{}{indicantur: 1) aera Nabonassaris, 2) aera Philippi Aridaei, 3) aera Diocletiani vel Martyrum (1).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0333-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L004}{}{}{\Indent Sed harum nullam vult \Name{al–Battānī}. Ex iis quae postea in hoc capite et in cap. XLII}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L005}{}{}{narrat, patet eam esse aeram, annis \textit{Iulianis} praeditam, cuius epocha est feria sexta (dies}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L006}{}{}{Veneris) 29 Augusti 25 ante Chr. natum, nam ab aera Dhū ’l–qarnayn distat 287 annis Iu-}
\par\vspace{0.12mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L007}{}{}{lianis minus 3 diebus.}
\par\vspace{-0.12mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0333-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L008}{}{}{\Indent Est ergo forma quaedam \textit{aerae Augusti} vel \textit{Actiacae.} Huius aerae fortuito meminit \Name{Pto-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L009}{}{}{\Name{lemaeus}, \textit{Almag.} III, 6 (ed. Halma t. I, p. 204), e quo loco \Name{Ideler}, \textit{Handbuch der ma-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L010}{}{}{\textit{thematischen und technischen Chronologie,} Berlin 1825–26, t. I, p. 116, deduxit eius epo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L011}{}{}{cham fuisse 1 thōth 719 Nabonassaris, i. e. diem dominicam 31 Augusti 30 ante Chr. natum,}
\par\vspace{0.17mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L012}{}{}{eiusque annos annos \textit{vagos} Aegyptios (2).}
\par\vspace{-0.14mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0333-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L013}{}{}{\Indent Differentia quam in epocha aerae \Name{al–Battānī} habet, hoc modo mihi explicanda videtur.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L014}{}{}{Narrat \Name{Theon} in \textit{Tabulis Manualibus} (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 30–32; cfr. \Name{Ideler} I, 158 sqq.)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L015}{}{}{primum diem mensis thōth anni vagi Aegyptii rursus cum primo die thōth anni fixi Alexan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L016}{}{}{drini (vel Iuliani) congruere post periodum 1460 annorum fixorum, vel 1461 annorum vago-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L017}{}{}{rum; hoc evenisse « \textit{quinto Augusti anno} » (ita p. 30) aut « \textit{quinque annis post initium}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L018}{}{}{\textit{regni Augusti} » (ita p. 32). Qua re, addit Theon, ab hoc quinto anno initium sumitur cum}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L019}{}{}{dies anni vagi converti debent in dies anni fixi Alexandrini.}
\par\vspace{-0.12mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0333-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L020}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Theonis} locus duas inter se discrepantes notitias praebet. Nam si periodus illa quinto Au-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L021}{}{}{gusti anno absoluta est, epocha aerae Theonis fit \textit{sexta feria (dies Veneris) 30 Augusti}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L022}{}{}{\textit{26} ante Christum natum; et ita fuisse cogitant, multis historicis argumentis nisi, \Name{Mommsen},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L023}{}{}{\Name{Lepsius} et \Name{Th. H. Martin} (3). Si autem periodus absoluta est 5 annis post initium regni}
\par\vspace{0.12mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L024}{}{}{Augusti, epocha aerae fit \textit{sexta feria 29 Aug. 25} ante Chr. natum; et hoc modo, sed non}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L025}{}{}{bene, intellexerunt \Name{Halma}, \Name{Biot}, \Name{Letronne} e recentioribus, \Name{al–Battānī} e veteribus.}
\par\vspace{0.25mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L026}{}{}{Anni huius aerae Iuliani sunt.}
\par\vspace{-0.24mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0333-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L027}{}{}{\Indent Copti igitur qui hac utebantur aera (\textit{Alexandrina} potius quam \textit{Augusti} vocanda), an-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L028}{}{}{num die 29 Augusti incipientes, tribus diebus, ut \Name{al–Battānī} ait, Graecos–Aegyptios prae-}
\par\vspace{0.15mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L029}{}{}{cedebant, quorum primus anni dies erat 1 Septembris.}
\par\vspace{-0.12mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0333-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L030}{}{}{\Indent Addit \Name{al–Battānī} quarto quoque anno differentiam non \textit{trium} sed \textit{duorum} tantum ex-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L031}{}{}{stare dierum: « qui [i. e. Graeci–Aegyptii] contra illos [i. e. Coptos] quarto quoque anno una}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-body-L032}{}{}{« die praecurrunt ». Huius rei explicationem invenio in fragmento chronologico \Name{Heraclii}}
\par\vspace{0.29mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{0.77mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0333-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Est aera Nabonassaris et aera Philippi apud}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Name{al-Farghānī}, ed. Golii, cap. I, p. 6; Diocletiani}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_left-L003}{}{}{apud \Name{Ḥabash} f. 4,v., \Name{Ibn Yūnus} (\Name{Delambre}}
\par\vspace{-0.14mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_left-L004}{}{}{96), \Name{al-Maqrīzī} (\textit{al-Mawā‘iẓ wa ’l-i‘tibār fī}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\textit{dhikr al-khiṭaṭ wa ’l–āthār}, Būlāq 1270 heg., t. I,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_left-L006}{}{}{p 261–262); aera Nabonassaris apud \Name{al-Maṣ‘ūdī},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_left-L007}{}{}{\textit{Prairies} t. III, p. 400; Nabonassaris, Philippi et Dio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_left-L008}{}{}{cletiani apud \Name{al-Mas‘ūdī}, \textit{Tanbīh} p. 198. —}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_left-L009}{}{}{\Name{Abū ’l-Faẓl ‘Allāmī} eam esse antiquam tan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_left-L010}{}{}{tum dicit (t. I, p. 274 $=$ vers. II, 23).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0333-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_left-L011}{}{}{\Indent (2) \Name{Petavius} et liber \textit{L’art de vérifier les}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_left-L012}{}{}{\textit{dates} habent 29 Aug. 30 ante Chr., quoniam male}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_left-L013}{}{}{putant annos aerae iam ab initio annos Iulianos}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{0.51mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_right-L001}{}{}{fuisse. — Ad primum Augusti annum \Name{Ptolemaeus}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_right-L002}{}{}{media loca Solis, Lunae et planetarum refert in \textit{In-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\textit{scriptione Canopi}, quae Hypotheses sequitur (\textit{Hy-}}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\textit{pothèses de} C. \Name{Ptolémée}, \textit{publiées et traduites}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\textit{par} \Name{Halma}, Paris 1820, p. 59).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0333-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\Indent (3) Qui quaestionem fuse egit in suo \textit{Sur la}}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\textit{date historique d’un renouvellement de la période}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_right-L008}{}{}{\textit{sothiaque}, p. 239-244 et 293-294 (in Mémoires pré-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_right-L009}{}{}{sentés par divers savants à l’Académie des Inscri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_right-L010}{}{}{ptions, 1\textsuperscript{e} série, t. VIII, 1\textsuperscript{e} partie, 1869). Implicite hanc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_right-L011}{}{}{opinionem sequitur R. \Name{Schram}, \textit{Hilfstafeln für}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_right-L012}{}{}{\textit{Chronologie} (in Denkschriften der kais. Akad. der}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0333-notes_right-L013}{}{}{Wissenschaften zu Wien, mathem. Cl., t. XLV, 1892).}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0334}{245}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 0.08mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L001}{}{}{imperatoris quod edidit Halma (\textit{Commentaires de} \Name{Théon} \textit{sur les tables manuelles,} t. III,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L002}{}{}{p. 106). Narrat enim « Alexandrinos diem intercalarem in fine anni qui bisextilem Romanum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L003}{}{}{« praecedat ponere; tunc ergo suum annum incipere non tertio sed secundo die tantum ante}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L004}{}{}{« Septembrem ». \Name{Al–Battānī} hunc usum manifeste vult, qui fusius ab \Name{al–Bīrūnī}, \textit{Chron.},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L005}{}{}{p. 141 et 196 text. (137 et 176 vers.) exponitur, et hodie etiam apud Coptos viget, qua re}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L006}{}{}{post annum intercalarem Copticum usque ad Februarii finem anni bisextilis Gregoriani, con-}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L007}{}{}{cordantia mensium una die mutatur (1):}
\par\vspace{2.26mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L008}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{37.60mm}\smash{\rlap{1}}}\rlap{\hspace*{42.19mm}\smash{\rlap{tūt}}}\rlap{\hspace*{59.11mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{67.22mm}\smash{\llap{11}}}\rlap{\hspace*{69.89mm}\smash{\rlap{Sept.;}}}\rlap{\hspace*{83.26mm}\smash{\rlap{in}}}\rlap{\hspace*{89.05mm}\smash{\rlap{annis}}}\rlap{\hspace*{101.26mm}\smash{\rlap{intercalaribus:}}}\rlap{\hspace*{136.58mm}\smash{\llap{12}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.12mm}\smash{\rlap{Sept.}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L009}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{37.60mm}\smash{\rlap{1}}}\rlap{\hspace*{42.19mm}\smash{\rlap{bābeh}}}\rlap{\hspace*{59.11mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{67.22mm}\smash{\llap{11}}}\rlap{\hspace*{69.89mm}\smash{\rlap{Oct.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{85.00mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{93.55mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{114.75mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{136.58mm}\smash{\llap{12}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.12mm}\smash{\rlap{Oct.}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L010}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{37.60mm}\smash{\rlap{1}}}\rlap{\hspace*{42.19mm}\smash{\rlap{hatūr}}}\rlap{\hspace*{59.11mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{67.22mm}\smash{\llap{10}}}\rlap{\hspace*{69.89mm}\smash{\rlap{Nov.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{85.00mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{93.55mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{114.75mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{136.58mm}\smash{\llap{11}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.12mm}\smash{\rlap{Nov.}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L011}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{37.60mm}\smash{\rlap{1}}}\rlap{\hspace*{42.19mm}\smash{\rlap{kiyahk}}}\rlap{\hspace*{59.11mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{67.22mm}\smash{\llap{10}}}\rlap{\hspace*{69.89mm}\smash{\rlap{Dec.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{85.00mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{93.55mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{114.75mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{136.58mm}\smash{\llap{11}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.12mm}\smash{\rlap{Dec.}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L012}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{37.60mm}\smash{\rlap{1}}}\rlap{\hspace*{42.19mm}\smash{\rlap{ṭūbah}}}\rlap{\hspace*{59.11mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{67.22mm}\smash{\llap{9}}}\rlap{\hspace*{69.89mm}\smash{\rlap{Ian.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{85.00mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{93.55mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{114.75mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{136.58mm}\smash{\llap{10}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.12mm}\smash{\rlap{Ian.}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L013}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{37.60mm}\smash{\rlap{1}}}\rlap{\hspace*{42.19mm}\smash{\rlap{amshīr}}}\rlap{\hspace*{59.11mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{67.22mm}\smash{\llap{8}}}\rlap{\hspace*{69.89mm}\smash{\rlap{Febr.}}}\rlap{\hspace*{85.00mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{93.55mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{114.75mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{»}}}\rlap{\hspace*{136.58mm}\smash{\llap{9}}}\rlap{\hspace*{139.12mm}\smash{\rlap{Febr.}}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L014}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{70.34mm}\smash{\rlap{1}}}\rlap{\hspace*{74.97mm}\smash{\rlap{barmahāt}}}\rlap{\hspace*{96.80mm}\smash{\makebox[0pt][c]{$=$}}}\rlap{\hspace*{105.00mm}\smash{\llap{10}}}\rlap{\hspace*{107.49mm}\smash{\rlap{Mart.}}}}
\par\vspace{2.54mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0334-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L015}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Martin}, l. c., p. 282–283, usus Alexandrini originem hoc modo explicat: probabile est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L016}{}{}{tetraëteridem seu quadriennium Alexandrinum imitari quadriennium Sothiacum quod fortasse}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L017}{}{}{quartum annum bisextilem haberet; tempore autem quo annus fixus Alexandrinus constitutus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L018}{}{}{sit, bisextilem annum Sothiacum fuisse tertium annum quadriennii Iuliani.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0334-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L019}{}{}{\Indent Aera Augusti cuiuslibet speciei, tam Ptolemaicae quam Theonianae, haud longo tempore}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L020}{}{}{in ipso Aegypto viguit. Itaque raro ab Orientalibus scriptoribus memoratur, nec semper, ut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L021}{}{}{videtur, recte. Ex iis quae \Name{Ḥabash}, f. 3,r. et 5,r., atque \Name{al–Bīrūnī}, \textit{Chron.}, p. 29, 137,}
\par\vspace{-0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L022}{}{}{141 et 196 text. (33, 133, 137, 176 vers.), de aera Augusti (\textit{ta’rīkh Oghusṭos}) dicunt, patet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L023}{}{}{eos locum Theonis eodem quo al–Battānī modo interpretatos esse, et epocham aerae die 29 Aug.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L024}{}{}{25 ante Chr. n. posuisse. Sed \Name{Kūshiyār al–Ǵīlī} (2), \Name{Ibn Yūnus} (3), \Name{al–Khāzinī} (\textit{az–}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L025}{}{}{\textit{–zīǵ as-sanǵarī}, lib. I, sect. I, cap. II, fol. 22,v.), et \Name{al–Kharaqī} (tract. III, cap. VI, cod.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L026}{}{}{Paris. f. 118,v.) aeram Augusti ita supputant ut eius epocham deducatur esse procul dubio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L027}{}{}{feriam quintam (diem Iovis) 13 Novembris 30 ante Chr. n.; ne quidem igitur cum Ptolemaei}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L028}{}{}{feria prima (die dominica) 31 Aug. 30 ante Chr. convenit. Causam huius differentiae nescio.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L029}{}{}{\Name{Abū ’l–faẓl ‘Allāmī} (\textit{Āyīn–i–Akbarī,} t. I, p. 275 $=$ II, 25 vers) tantum ait aeram Augusti}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L030}{}{}{ab initio regni (i. e. anno 30 ante Chr. n.) incipere, et eius annos esse Iulianos, menses vero}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L031}{}{}{Copticos.}
\par\vspace{5.31mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0334-H32}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L032}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{c}}) — \textit{Pag. 70, l. 18–23.}}}
\par\vspace{4.34mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0334-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L033}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Primum diem dati anni Persici} (iuxta aeram Iezdegerdis) supputare in quem diem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L034}{}{}{anni Romani aerae Dhū ’l–qarnayn (vel Seleucidarum) incidat. — De 366 demantur, ait \Name{al–}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L035}{}{}{\Name{–Battānī}, 77 dies $+$ tot dies quot annos Romanos complectitur quarta pars annorum aerae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L036}{}{}{Dhū ’l–qarnayn post epocham Iezdegerdis elapsorum; quod e subductione restat, incipiendo a}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-body-L037}{}{}{primo die aylūl (Septembris) supputetur; exibit primus anni Persici dies.}
\par\vspace{0.34mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{0.26mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0334-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Numeri sequentes a 1 Mart. 1900 ad 28 Fe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-notes_left-L002}{}{}{bruar. 2100 inserviunt, quo temporis intervallo ca-}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-notes_left-L003}{}{}{lendarium Gregorianum 13 diebus Iulianum praece-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-notes_left-L004}{}{}{dit. — Copti hodierni aera Diocletiani vel Martyrum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-notes_left-L005}{}{}{utuntur, cuius epocha feria sexta 29 Aug. 284 Chr.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0334-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (2) Apud \Name{Ideler}, \textit{Handb. d. mathem. Chron.},}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{0.18mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-notes_right-L001}{}{}{II, 628, 632-633. — Scripsit anno circiter 400 heg.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-notes_right-L002}{}{}{(1009-1010 Chr.); v. \Name{Suter}, \textit{Die Mathem.}, p. 83-84,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-notes_right-L003}{}{}{nr. 192.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0334-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (3) In I cap., apud \Name{Delambre} p. 96, ubi, in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-notes_right-L005}{}{}{lin. 5 a fine pag, 241287 est error typothetae pro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0334-notes_right-L006}{}{}{241281.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0335}{246}
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 0.08mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0335-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent Regula inserviet usque quo residuum 0 inveniatur, id est usque quo oriatur aequatio}
\par\vspace{1.14mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L002}{}{}{366 $=$ 77 $+$ $\smash{\dfrac{x}{\text{4}}}$, unde deducitur $\smash{\dfrac{x}{\text{4}}}$ $=$ 366 $-$ 77 $=$ 289, et $x$ $=$ 289 $\times$ 4 $=$ 1156 annis}
\par\vspace{1.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L003}{}{}{Iulianis. Itaque regula adhiberi potest usque ad primum diem anni Iuliani 1156 Iezdegerdis $=$}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L004}{}{}{1788 Chr., quod Albatenii lectoribus optime sufficiebat.}
\par\vspace{1.09mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0335-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L005}{}{}{\Indent Iam 1156 annis Romanis post Iezdegerdem elapsis, scilicet residuo 366 $-$ $\smash{\left(\text{77} + \dfrac{x}{\text{4}}\right)}$ ne-}
\par\vspace{1.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L006}{}{}{gativo exstante, cum hoc residuo a 1 aylūl (Septembri) non procedendum sed regredien-}
\par\vspace{0.18mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L007}{}{}{dum erit.}
\par\vspace{3.19mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0335-H08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L008}{}{}{\CenterLine{A\textsc{d} \textsc{caput} XXXIII.}}
\par\vspace{3.49mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0335-H09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L009}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 71, l. 14–15.}}}
\par\vspace{3.32mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0335-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L010}{}{}{\Indent [« \textit{Annum autem in quo es cum iis non numera nisi meridies ultimi diei subāṭ}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L011}{}{}{[Februarii] \textit{iam transierit} ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0335-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L012}{}{}{\Indent Ut incommodum vitaret qui oritur e die intercalari in medio anni posito, \Name{al–Battānī}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L013}{}{}{epochas tabularum constituit ad annum fictum, cuius initium non est 1 aylūl (Septembris) sed}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L014}{}{}{1 ādhār (Martii) praecedentis; primum mensem igitur in tabulis menstruorum motuum (t. II,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L015}{}{}{p. 74, 87, 104, 107) semper ādhār, non aylūl posuit. Hoc modo in eius anno ficto dies interca-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L016}{}{}{laris est ultimus anni; numeri qui in tabulis respondent singulis mensibus immutati manent}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L017}{}{}{tam in bisextilibus quam in communibus annis, subāṭ excepto quem tamen adhibere nunquam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L018}{}{}{necesse est, nam, subāṭ perfecto, annus quoque perficitur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0335-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L019}{}{}{\Indent Epochae igitur mediorum motuum ad singulos annos in t. II, p. 72 tabularum, non ad ini-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L020}{}{}{tium aylūl illius anni sunt, sed ad meridiem primi diei ādhār (Martii) praecedentis. Quod}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L021}{}{}{melius patebit si consideretur pro anno 1191 Dhū ’l–qarnayn longitudinem Solis 342° 48′ 50″}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L022}{}{}{indicari, quod nunquam evenire potest primo die Septembris in supputatione Iuliana, nisi tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L023}{}{}{pora longe remotissima ab \Name{al–Battānī} et a nobis cogitari velint.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0335-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent Animadvertendum est quoque in t. II, p. 72 longitudinem mediam Solis anno 1211 Dhū}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L025}{}{}{’l–qarnayn constare numero integro graduum, sine minutis et secundis; qua re puto \Name{al–Bat-}}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L026}{}{}{\Name{tānī}, in tabula sua condenda, ab \textit{anno 1211} Dhū ’l–qarnayn, tamquam ab epocha fundamen-}
\par\vspace{0.00mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L027}{}{}{tali, profectum esse, et ex ea reliquas epochas auxilio mediorum motuum deduxisse. — Pro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L028}{}{}{anomalia Lunae et motu nodi videtur contra epocham fundamentalem \textit{annum 1191} statuisse,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L029}{}{}{nam sub hoc anno numeri rotundi sunt. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{4.03mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0335-H30}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L030}{}{}{\CenterLine{A\textsc{d} \textsc{caput} XXXV.}}
\par\vspace{2.29mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0335-H31}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L031}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 73–74.}}}
\par\vspace{3.44mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0335-P07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Supputatio duodecim domorum astrologicarum.}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0335-P08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L033}{}{}{\Indent Quae \Name{Delambre} p. 45–48 habet, ad hoc caput explicandum non inserviunt, nam domo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L034}{}{}{rum divisio a \Name{Delambre} delecta non est ea quam \Name{al–Battānī} vult. Quid hae domus sint}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L035}{}{}{breviter antea exponam, praesertim duce V. \Name{Nabod}, \textit{Enarratio elementorum astrologiae,}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L036}{}{}{\textit{in qua praeter} \Name{Alcabicii}, \textit{qui Arabum doctrinam in compendio prodidit, expositionem}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L037}{}{}{\textit{atque cum Ptolemaei principiis collationem, de verae artis praeceptorum origine et usu}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-body-L038}{}{}{\textit{satis disseritur,} Coloniae 1560, p. 116–122 (1).}
\par\vspace{1.11mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{1.36mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0335-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Est longus commentarius, sed mathematicis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-notes_left-L002}{}{}{demonstrationibus destitutus, ad notissimum libel-}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{1.42mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-notes_right-L001}{}{}{lum \Name{Alcabitii} (‘Abd al-‘Azīz \Name{al-Qabīṣī}), qui}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0335-notes_right-L002}{}{}{saeculo X Chr. floruit; \Name{Suter}, p. 60-61, nr. 132.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0336}{247}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0336-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent Diversae sunt apud astrologos rationes, quibus universum Caelum in duodecim partes,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L002}{}{}{\textit{domos} (1) appellatas, varie dividatur; omnes tamen conveniunt in hoc quod sex circulos ma-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L003}{}{}{ximos in duobus oppositis locis se mutuo secantes, et quorum communis diametrus ab austro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L004}{}{}{ad septentriones tendat, cogitantur universum Caelum in duodecim partes distinguere. Sed extre-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L005}{}{}{mitates diametri, in quibus sex illi circuli se mutuo secant, alii astrologi aliter constituunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0336-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L006}{}{}{\Indent \Name{Alcabitius} et qui ante \Name{Campanum} (XIII Chr. saec.) de domiciliis caelestibus partiendis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L007}{}{}{scripserunt (ut \Name{Nabod} narrat), haec duo puncta esse aequatoris polos existimaverunt. Duo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L008}{}{}{circuli maximi sint, quorum alter meridianus, alter qui per punctum eclipticae in horizonte}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L009}{}{}{situm transit et aequatorem ad angulos rectos secat. \textit{Circulus aequinoctialis} hoc modo in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L010}{}{}{quatuor partes divisus apparet, quarum binae, sibi invicem oppositae, aequales. Omnes hae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L011}{}{}{quatuor partes trifariam aequaliterque secentur, et per puncta sectionum et polos aequatoris}
\par\vspace{-0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L012}{}{}{ducantur quatuor circuli (ergo quatuor \textit{declinationis circuli}) qui cum duobus prioribus duo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L013}{}{}{decim domus conficient (2). Ita ecliptica in duodecim partes dividitur, quarum nonnisi oppositae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L014}{}{}{sunt aequales; semel tantum in die ecliptica in quatuor aequales partes divisa apparebit, ni-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L015}{}{}{mirum temporis momento quo initia Arietis et Librae in horizontem inciderint.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0336-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L016}{}{}{\Indent Alii extremitates diametri communis posuerunt esse puncta intersectionis horizontis et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L017}{}{}{meridiani; reliqui quatuor circuli maximi sunt igitur qui \textit{circuli positionis} vocantur. Sed de}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L018}{}{}{ratione hos circulos describendi, ut \Name{Nabod} narrat, certant (3):}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0336-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L019}{}{}{\Indent\hspace*{.8em}\textit{a}) \Name{Campanus} et \Name{Gazulus} (4) circulum \textit{verticalem} sumunt qui per puncta ortus et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L020}{}{}{occasus transit atque a meridiano et horizonte in quatuor aequales quadrantes dividitur; sin-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L021}{}{}{gulos quadrantes verticalis in ternas sectiones aequales partiuntur, et per sectionum puncta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L022}{}{}{\textit{circulos positionis} ducunt. Ita duodecim domus sunt duodecim fusi aequales.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0336-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L023}{}{}{\Indent\hspace*{.8em}\textit{b}) \Name{Regiomontanus}, in XIV problemate \textit{Tabularum directionum,} ponit circulum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L024}{}{}{\textit{aequinoctialem} in aequales quadrantes dividi circulo meridiano et horizonte obliquo; qua-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L025}{}{}{drantes postea in ternas partes aequales, 30 graduum, secari, et per sectionum puncta \textit{circulos}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L026}{}{}{\textit{positionis} duci.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0336-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L027}{}{}{\Indent Alii demum, ut \Name{Iohannes Schöner} (5), \textit{eclipticam} in quatuor quadrantes dividi exi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L028}{}{}{stimant horizonte et circulo maximo per ambos eclipticae polos et per zenith transeunte; qua-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L029}{}{}{drantes in ternas partes aequales secari; per puncta sectionum et eclipticae polos quatuor \textit{cir-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L030}{}{}{\textit{culos} alios \textit{latitudinis} duci; ita eclipticam et sphaeram caelestem in duodenas domus aequales}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L031}{}{}{dividi. « Ad quas omnes astrologos sua iudicia accommodasse », ait \Name{Nabod}, « a multis pro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L032}{}{}{« ditum est. Id quod verum esse negari non potest, si de \Name{Firmico}, \Name{Albohali} (6), \Name{Albu-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L033}{}{}{« \Name{masare} (7), \Name{Albubatere} (8), \Name{Bonato} (9) et similibus intelligatur ». Sed \Name{Ptolemaeum}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-body-L034}{}{}{\textit{aequatorem} in 12 partes aequales (30 graduum, scilicet binarum horarum aequinoctialium)}
\par\vspace{2.67mm}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}

\par\vspace{2.31mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0336-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Vel \textit{domicilia}; Graece \textgreek{τόποι} « loca »; apud}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Name{Firmicum} \textit{loca}; apud Arabes \textit{bayt} (pl. \textit{buyūt})}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-notes_left-L003}{}{}{« domus », perraro \textit{makān} (pl. \textit{amkinah}) « loca ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0336-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (2) Male \Name{Delambre}, p. 501, putare videtur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Name{Alcabitium} circulis positionis usum esse.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0336-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (3) Priores tres rationes fuse exponit \Name{Regio-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-notes_left-L007}{}{}{\Name{montanus} (\Name{Iohannis de Monteregio} \textit{Tabu-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-notes_left-L008}{}{}{\textit{lae directionum profectionumque,} Venetiis 1485,}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-notes_left-L009}{}{}{probl. XIV-XVI).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0336-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-notes_left-L010}{}{}{\Indent (4) Iohannes Gazulus, Ragusinus, fuit ineunte}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-notes_left-L011}{}{}{XV saec.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0336-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-notes_left-L012}{}{}{\Indent (5) Natus 1477, mortuus 1547. De domiciliis cae-}
\end{minipage}

\par\vspace{2.35mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-notes_right-L001}{}{}{lestibus dissertatio est in fine etiam eius operis quod}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\textit{Tabulae resolutae astronomicae} I. \Name{Schoneri},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-notes_right-L003}{}{}{Witenbergae 1587, inscribitur.}
\par\vspace{-0.13mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0336-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (6) I. e. Abū ‘Alī Yaḥyà \Name{al-Khayyāṭ}, ex-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-notes_right-L005}{}{}{eunte VIII et ineunte IX Chr saeculo floruit; \Name{Su-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\Name{ter} p. 9-10, nr. 17.}
\par\vspace{0.30mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0336-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0336-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\Indent (7) \Name{Abū Ma‘shar}, m. 28 ramaḍān 272 heg.}

\DSource{0337}{248}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L001}{}{}{divisisse, \Name{Nabod} e diversis Tetrabibli locis contra aliorum auctorum opinionem deducere}
\par\vspace{0.14mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L002}{}{}{conatur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0337-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L003}{}{}{\Indent Haec duodecim \textit{eclipticae} puncta, quacumque ratione constituta, \textit{cuspides} aut \textit{initia do-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L004}{}{}{\textit{morum} appellant. Ex iis quatuor praesertim maximi momenti, quibus nomen \textgreek{κέντρα, γωνίαι},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L005}{}{}{\textit{cardines geniturae, anguli,} et, Arabice, \textit{paxilli} (\textit{watad} pl. \textit{awtād}); sunt 1) \textit{ascendens, pars}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L006}{}{}{\textit{horoscopans} (\textgreek{ἀνατολή, ἀνατέλλων, μοῖρα ὡροσκοποῦσα, ὡροσκόπος}, \textit{ṭāli‘}), scilicet punctum ecli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L007}{}{}{pticae in horizonte orientali, dato temporis momento; 2) \textit{imum Caelum} (\textgreek{ὑπόγειον, ἀντιμεσου}-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L008}{}{}{\textgreek{ράνημα, ἀντιμεσουρανῶν, ὑποχθόνιον κέντρον}, \textit{watad al–arḍ} « paxillus Terrae », \textit{ar–rābi‘} « quar-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L009}{}{}{tus »), nimirum eclipticae punctum in meridiano inferiore; 3) \textit{occasus} (\textgreek{δύσις, δύνων}, \textit{al–ghārib}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L010}{}{}{« occidens »), seu eclipticae punctum in horizonte occidentali; 4) \textit{medium Caelum} (\textgreek{μεσουρά}-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L011}{}{}{\textgreek{νημα, μεσουρανῶν}, \textit{watad wasaṭ as–samā’} « paxillus medii Caeli », \textit{al–‘āshir} « decimus »), quod}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L012}{}{}{est locus intersectionis eclipticae et meridiani superioris. In quacumque divisione ordo paxil-}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L013}{}{}{lorum et domorum semper idem est, scilicet ad successionem signorum.}
\par\vspace{0.01mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0337-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L014}{}{}{\Indent De cuiusque domus virtutibus libri astrologiae consulantur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0337-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L015}{}{}{\Indent Ut ex ipsis supputationis praeceptis apparet, divisio domorum \Name{Albateniana} procul du-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L016}{}{}{bio ea est quam \Name{Alcabitius} et Iudaeus \Name{Ibn ‘Ezrā} (Avenezra, apud \Name{Nabod} l. c.) docent;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L017}{}{}{eandem invenio apud \Name{Ḥabash} f. 102,r. et 108,v., \Name{Abū ’l–Wafā’} (1), \Name{Oloug Beg} p. 145–}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L018}{}{}{–146 text. (195–196 vers.), et passim in \textit{Libros del saber de astronomia} quos ex Arabicis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L019}{}{}{fontibus rex Alphonsus X conflari iussit. Eam in animo habuisse \Name{Ptolemaeus} in \textit{Tetrabi-}}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L020}{}{}{\textit{blo} videtur; cfr. \Name{Nabod} l. c. et quae infra e \textit{Libros del saber} adducam.}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.429\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0337-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0337-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.551\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.449\FullSourceWidth}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0337-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L021}{}{}{\Indent Sint PSP′N circulus meridianus, NES horizon}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L022}{}{}{(ubi N punctum boreale, S australe, E orientale),}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L023}{}{}{BB′ aequator caelestis, A{\MoonFont ♈}X ecliptica, {\MoonFont ♈} initium}
\par\vspace{0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L024}{}{}{Arietis, P et P′ aequatoris poli, A punctum eclipti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L025}{}{}{cae tunc in horizonte orientali exstans scilicet ascen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L026}{}{}{dens. — Iubet ante omnia \Name{al–Battānī} horas tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L027}{}{}{porales puncti A (2) sumere (« \textit{tempora horaria}}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L028}{}{}{« \textit{gradus ascendentis in eo climate} ») easque du-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L029}{}{}{plicare; quod idem est ac aequatoris arcum \textit{a}B′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L030}{}{}{(arcum semidiurnum puncti A), \textit{a}B (arcum semino-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L031}{}{}{cturnum) eorumque oppositos in hemisphaerio occi-}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L032}{}{}{dentali dividere in ternas partes aequales. Circulis}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.40mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L033}{}{}{declinationis per loca sectionum ductis, duae series inaequales fusorum oriuntur; quarum in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L034}{}{}{altera fusi binas horas temporales diurnas ascendentis complectuntur, in altera binas horas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L035}{}{}{nocturnas. Aequator sectus apparet in \textit{a}, 2, 3, 4 ..... 10, 11, 12; ecliptica in A (ascendente), II,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L036}{}{}{III, IV (paxillo Terrae) ..... X (medio Caelo), XI, XII, quae sunt initia vel cuspides domorum.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L037}{}{}{Arcubus aequatoris cognitis, tabulae ascensionum rectarum statim dabunt arcus eclipticae cor-}
\par\vspace{-0.15mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L038}{}{}{respondentes.}
\par\vspace{0.20mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0337-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L039}{}{}{\Indent Cognitio arcus semidiurni et seminocturni ascendentis est cognitio quoque ascensionum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L040}{}{}{rectarum paxillorum, i. e. cuspidum I, IV, VII, X domus. Reliquae regulis \Name{Albatenianis} (3)}
\par\vspace{-0.13mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-body-L041}{}{}{invenientur:}
\par\vspace{2.10mm}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\textit{’l-Wéfa al-Bûzdjâni} (Journal Asiatique, ser. VIII,}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0337-notes_right-L001}{}{}{horas Solis ut \Name{Delambre} p. 45 putat. — \textsc{Schia-}}

\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 1.46mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L001}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{93.49mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{\ARsign{} initii XI domus $=$ \ARsign{} medii Caeli $+ \dfrac{\text{arc. semid. ascendentis}}{3}$}}}
\par\vspace{-0.20mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L002}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{93.17mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{\ARsign{} initii XII domus $=$ \ARsign{} initii XI $+ \dfrac{\text{arc. semid. ascendentis}}{3}$}}}
\par\vspace{-0.50mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L003}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{93.38mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{\ARsign{} initii II domus $=$ \ARsign{} ascendentis $+ \left(\text{60°} - \dfrac{\text{arc. semid. ascendentis}}{3}\right)$}}}
\par\vspace{-1.54mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L004}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{94.26mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{\ARsign{} initii III domus $=$ \ARsign{} initii II $+ \left(\text{60°} - \dfrac{\text{arc. semid. ascendentis}}{3}\right)$}}}
\par\vspace{-0.42mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L005}{}{}{Initia V, VI, VIII, IX sunt nadir XI, XII, II, III.}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{118.90mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0338-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L006}{}{}{\Indent In hac domorum partitione igitur non solum duae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L007}{}{}{series inaequales fusorum oriuntur, sed etiam absurditas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L008}{}{}{reperitur quam his verbis \Name{Delambre}, p. 502 de Alca-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L009}{}{}{bitio loquens, notat (1): « Les six maisons dernières sont}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L010}{}{}{« toujours diamétralement opposées aux six premières; il}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L011}{}{}{« en résulte cependant une espèce d’absurdité. Le quart}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L012}{}{}{« de l’équateur, entre le méridien et l’horizon occidental,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L013}{}{}{« se trouve divisé suivant les arcs nocturnes, quoiqu’il}
\par\vspace{0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L014}{}{}{« appartienne au jour; le quart entre l’horizon occidental}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L015}{}{}{« et le méridien inférieur, est divisé suivant les heures}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L016}{}{}{« du jour, quoiqu’il appartienne à la nuit. Au reste, le}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L017}{}{}{« calcul de cette méthode est extrêmement simple, et c’est}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{67.10mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0338-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0338-F01}\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.36mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L018}{}{}{« peut–être ce qui a fait passer sur l’absurdité que nous venons de remarquer ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0338-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L019}{}{}{\Indent Supputandi ratio \Name{Albateniana} reperitur quoque in \textit{Libros del saber de Astronomia}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L020}{}{}{aut nomine \Name{Ptolemaei} aut \Name{al–Battānī}. Nam t. II, p. 60–61 (\textit{Libro de las armellas}, lib. II,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L021}{}{}{cap. XLIII): « De saber yguar las XII casas segund la opinion de Ptolomeo et de el Bateni »;}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L022}{}{}{t. II, p. 193 (\textit{Libro del astrolabio redondo,} libro II, cap. LIX): « De saber ygualar las doze}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L023}{}{}{« casas segun la opinion de Ptolomeo et de Veles » (2); t. III, p. 71 (\textit{Libros de la lámina}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L024}{}{}{\textit{universal,} pars II, cap. LXII): « de saber ygualar las casas que caen entre los ángulos, se-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L025}{}{}{« gund la opinion de Ptolomeo »; t. III, p. 204 (\textit{Libro de la açafeha,} cuius auctor \Name{az–Zar-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L026}{}{}{\Name{qālī}, cap. LVI): « De saber eguar las casas segund la oppinion de Ptholomeo », supputatio ab}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L027}{}{}{Albateniana non differt (3). Nec diversae sunt regulae quas \Name{Ḥabash} et \Name{Oloug Beg} (in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L028}{}{}{initio capitis) docent.}
\par\vspace{0.30mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0338-H29}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L029}{}{}{\CenterLine{A\textsc{d} \textsc{caput} XXXVI.}}
\par\vspace{1.83mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0338-H30}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L030}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 75, l. 27-30.}}}
\par\vspace{3.31mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0338-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L031}{}{}{\Indent [Quae de Luna dicit vera sunt; Solem quod attinet addere debebat pro numeris 95° et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L032}{}{}{265° ponendum esse 90 $+$ maxima aequatio centri, et 270 $-$ maxima aequatio centri; ergo,}
\par\vspace{-0.14mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-body-L033}{}{}{in numeris rotundis, 92° et 268°.}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{0.59mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-notes_left-L001}{}{}{vel arcus semidiurnus eius nadir. Qua re si arcus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-notes_left-L002}{}{}{semidiurnus cuiuslibet eclipticae puncti addatur ar-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-notes_left-L003}{}{}{cui semidiurno eius nadir, semper 180° proveniunt;}
\par\vspace{-0.78mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-notes_left-L004}{}{}{item summa fusi maioris et fusi minoris $\dfrac{\text{180°}}{3} = \text{60°}$}
\par\vspace{-0.58mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-notes_left-L005}{}{}{semper erit. Unde praeceptum Albatenianum p. 74,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-notes_left-L006}{}{}{l. 9-10. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-notes_left-L009}{}{}{stis ostendat.}

\DLine{AB01-PDF0338-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Name{Valens} Antiochenus, astrologus celeberrimus cu-}

\par\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.278\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0339-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0339-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.702\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.298\FullSourceWidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0339-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent Cuius causa haec est. Motus medius, in figura hoc loco de-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L002}{}{}{scripta, est motus centri O epicycli; cum Sol est in I, scilicet in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L003}{}{}{linea epicyclo tangenti, motu medio quoque movetur, nam tunc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L004}{}{}{eius motus in epicyclo nec accelerat nec retardat velocitatem an-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L005}{}{}{gularem respectu Terrae T; idque evenit exstante arcu MI (i. e.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L006}{}{}{anomalia) $=$ 90° $+$ OTI $=$ 90° $+$ maxima aequatio centri. Similiter}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L007}{}{}{cum Sol in altera tangente est; tunc anomalia $=$ MIK $=$ 270° $-$}
\par\vspace{-0.07mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L008}{}{}{KTO $=$ 270° $-$ maxima aequatio centri. In Sole maxima aequatio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L009}{}{}{est 2° fere; itaque MI $=$ 92°, MIK $=$ 268°. In Luna, cuius ma-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L010}{}{}{xima aequatio in syzygiis 5° complectitur, MI $=$ 95°, MIK $=$ 265°}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L011}{}{}{ut \Name{al–Battānī} habet.}
\end{minipage}\par
\par\vspace{-0.41mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0339-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L012}{}{}{\Indent Quoad Lunam tamen animadverti debet maximam aequationem posse excentricitatis causa}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L013}{}{}{mutari et 7° 40′ attingere; qua re in quadraturis, ubi effectus excentricitatis maximus est,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L014}{}{}{fiunt MI $=$ 97° 40′, MIK $=$ 262° 20′. Omnes has considerationes nimio brevitatis studio \Name{al–}}
\par\vspace{-0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L015}{}{}{\Name{–Battānī} omisit. — \textsc{Schiaparelli}.]}
\par\vspace{6.44mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0339-H16}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L016}{}{}{\CenterLine{A\textsc{d} \textsc{caput} XXXVII.}}
\par\vspace{4.48mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0339-H17}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L017}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 75–76.}}}
\par\vspace{4.48mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0339-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L018}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Motus nodi} retrogradus est, qua re a 360° demendus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0339-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L019}{}{}{\Indent Nomina \textit{caput} et \textit{cauda} ignota astronomis Graecis, apud quos \textgreek{ὁ ἀναβιβάζων} [\textgreek{σύνδεσμος}]}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L020}{}{}{« ascendens [nodus] » est nodus borealis, \textgreek{ὁ καταβιβάζων} « descendens » australis. Caput et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L021}{}{}{cauda ad caelestem Draconem referuntur, quem e Chaldaeorum superstitionibus primi Christi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L022}{}{}{saeculi receperant Gnostici et astrologi Graeci quidam; cfr. A. \Name{Bouché–Leclercq}, \textit{L’astro-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L023}{}{}{\textit{logie grecque,} Paris 1899, p. 121–123.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0339-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent Vox \textit{ǵawzahar,} e Persico \textit{gawzahr} \ArabicRun{ڭوزهر}, significat: 1) circulum pareclipticum (cfr. p. 45,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L025}{}{}{adn. 3) Lunae seu orbitam lunarem; 2) nodos orbitae lunaris, et praesertim nodum ascenden-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L026}{}{}{tem; 3) nodos orbitae cuiuscumque planetae. Probabiliter est vox Zendica \textit{gaocithra} « semen}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L027}{}{}{« bovum [servans] », epitheton Lunae in libro \textit{Avestā} inscripto; putabant enim Lunam semen}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L028}{}{}{primi bovis servavisse, et ex eo varia armentorum genera procreavisse (1). Apud scriptores}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L029}{}{}{Latinos saeculi XI et seqq. nomen Arabicum corruptum in \textit{iozahar, iehuzar, gehuzahar,}}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L030}{}{}{\textit{geuzaar, genzahar, zeunzaar, çeuçahat} saepe invenitur; cfr. M. \Name{Steinschneider}, \textit{Let-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L031}{}{}{\textit{tera seconda a D. B. Boncompagni, intorno al libro} « \textit{Saraceni cuiusdam de eris} »,}
\par\vspace{-0.09mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L032}{}{}{Roma 1863, p. 15–16 et 19.}
\par\vspace{5.26mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0339-H33}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L033}{}{}{\CenterLine{A\textsc{d} \textsc{caput} XXXVIII.}}
\par\vspace{4.54mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0339-H34}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L034}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 76, l. 10-17.}}}
\par\vspace{4.54mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0339-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L035}{}{}{\Indent \textit{Argumentum latitudinis,} quod est arcus orbitae lunaris inter quoddam orbitae punctum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L036}{}{}{et Lunae locum dato temporis momento, \Name{Ptolemaeus} et \Name{Theon} (\textit{Tables manuelles} t. I,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-body-L037}{}{}{p. 55 et passim) numerant a \textit{termino boreali} (\textgreek{βόρειον πέρας}) scilicet a puncto orbis lunaris in}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}{\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont
\par\fontsize{9}{11.80}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.49mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0339-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-notes-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) F. \Name{Spiegel}, \textit{Erânische Alterthumskunde,} Leipzig 1871-1878, t. II, p. 70 (quem locum mihi}
\par\vspace{-0.14mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0339-notes-L002}{}{}{I. \Name{Pizzi} suppeditavit).}
}
\DSource{0340}{251}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L001}{}{}{quo maxima habetur latitudo borealis; 90° a nodo ascendenti (vel boreali) distat. \Name{Al–Bat-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L002}{}{}{\Name{tānī} contra, et reliqui Arabes, ab ipso nodo argumentum supputant, \Name{Indorum} usum sequen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L003}{}{}{tes; Indi autem in hoc probabiliter astrologos \Name{Chaldaeo–Graecos} sectati sunt, apud quos nodi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L004}{}{}{Lunae, et praecipue nodus borealis, magnae erat auctoritatis et potestatis in genituris (vel}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L005}{}{}{genesibus), nam si planetae in eo benefici reperirentur genesis etiam mediocris et abiecta splen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L006}{}{}{didior et erecta evadebat. Ipsi astrologi saepe nodos orbis lunaris planetis adnumerant (1).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0340-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L007}{}{}{\Indent Formula \Name{Albateniana}}
\par\vspace{-1.06mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L008}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.34mm}(\textgreek{α})}\rlap{\hspace*{94.04mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{sin latit. \MoonSym{} $= \dfrac{\text{sin argum. lat. × sin maximae lat. \MoonSym{}}}{\text{\textit{R}}}$}}}
\par\vspace{0.54mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L009}{}{}{apud \Name{Ptolemaeum} et \Name{Theonem} non reperitur; simillima est regulae qua declinatio Solis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L010}{}{}{e longitudine et obliquitate eclipticae elicitur (pag. 12 et 162). In proiectione orthographica e}
\par\vspace{0.10mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L011}{}{}{similitudine duorum triangulorum facillime colligitur; nec differt a notissimo theoremate trian-}
\par\vspace{0.13mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L012}{}{}{gulorum sphaeralium rectangulorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L013}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{93.43mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{sin catheti : sin anguli oppositi $=$ sin hypotenusae : \textit{R}.}}}
\par\vspace{6.30mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0340-H14}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L014}{}{}{\CenterLine{A\textsc{d} \textsc{caput} XXXIX.}}
\par\vspace{3.49mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0340-H15}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L015}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{a}}) — \textit{Pag. 77, l, 23–24.}}}
\par\vspace{3.24mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0340-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L016}{}{}{\Indent A constructionibus geometricis \Name{Ptolemaei} V, 19 (ed. Halma t. I, p. 360–62), si A vo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L017}{}{}{cetur angulus quem ecliptica cum circulo verticali per Lunam transeunti efficit, aequationes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L018}{}{}{facile deducuntur quas \Name{al–Battānī} infra, p. 81, l. 4–6 enuntiat:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L019}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{94.17mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{parallaxis latitudinis $= \dfrac{\text{sin \textit{A} × parallaxis altitudinis}}{\text{\textit{R}}}$}}}
\par\vspace{0.64mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L020}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{94.29mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{parallaxis longitudinis $= \dfrac{\text{cos \textit{A} × parallaxis altitudinis}}{\text{\textit{R}}}\,.$}}}
\par\vspace{0.60mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L021}{}{}{E quibus consequitur regula hoc loco ab \Name{al–Battānī} data:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L022}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{0.26mm}(\textgreek{α})}\rlap{\hspace*{93.56mm}\makebox[0pt][c]{parall. latit. : parall. longit. $=$ sin \textit{A} : cos \textit{A}.}}}
\par\vspace{5.20mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0340-H23}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L023}{}{}{\CenterLine{\textbf{\textit{b}}) — \textit{Pag. 77, l. 25–27.}}}
\par\vspace{3.85mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0340-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent [« \textit{Parallaxis semper in eam latitudinis plagam declinat in qua est gradus} [\textit{eclip-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L025}{}{}{\textit{ticae}] \textit{quem} etc. ».}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{136.28mm}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0340-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L026}{}{}{\Indent Locus haud satis clarus et fortasse aliquantulum corruptus.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L027}{}{}{Si a zenith ducatur arcus circuli maximi ad punctum eclipticae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L028}{}{}{(aut potius paralleli latitudinis) in quo est Luna, parallaxis in la-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L029}{}{}{titudine fit australis aut borealis prout hic arcus ultra eclipticam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L030}{}{}{(aut parallelum latitudinis) productus ad plagam australem aut ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L031}{}{}{borealem tendit. Sit AB ecliptica (vel parallelus latitudinis), Z ze-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0340-body-L032}{}{}{nith, L locus Lunae; arcus ZL ultra AB producatur; Luna, quae}
\end{minipage}\hspace{2mm}\begin{minipage}[t]{49.72mm}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0340-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0340-F01}\end{minipage}\par
